# PrestasiPlus V3 — Release 1 development plan

**Status:** APPROVED by the user on 2026-11-04 (D111) — development may start; working model decided by the user (D110, 2026-11-04): (1) the UAT operator is a designated developer with server access and the user approves every write; (2) acceptance runs on UAT (a clone of V2 production data), there is no local acceptance environment; (3) content work (Bab mapping, figure images, regrouping) is done by ADMIN users in the Portal Pentadbiran. **Date:** 2026-11-04. **Owner:** Delivery Lead. **Language:** English (UI copy quoted in BM).
**Document set:** `docs/prd/r1/00-index.md` (entry) → `01-product-overview.md` (PRD/BRD) → `02-urs.md` (user requirements) → SRS per portal `10-murid.md`, `20-guru-admin-sekolah.md`, `30-admin-pusat.md` → `40-cross-cutting.md` (cross-cutting and NFR) → `50-data-model-erd.md` (DM) → `60-api-contract.md` (API) → this plan → `docs/plan/r1-traceability.md`. ID chain: URS → FR/AC → DM/API → plan card → AC ledger (`docs/plan/r1-ac-ledger.md`).
**Governing rule (user):** "berdasarkan V2+V1 untuk betulkan V3". V2 is the reference, V1 the fallback. Exception: question STRUCTURE follows D90/D41 and the DSKP (PRD 40 §X1, FR-X1-4).
**Inputs:** PRD `docs/prd/r1/` (00-index, 10-murid, 20-guru-admin-sekolah, 30-admin-pusat, 40-cross-cutting, **all FINAL**). Task cards use the real PRD IDs: `R1-MUR-<row>`, `FR-MUR-QP-n`, `R1-GUR-n`, `R1-AS-n`, `XS-n`, `R1-ADM-Q/C/N/U/M/P2-nn`, `QE-nn`, `U-OP-nn`; §4.0 reconciles them. Gap evidence: `docs/qa/gap-analysis-2026-11-04/`.

## 0. How to read this plan (one page)

1. The work is cut into **11 non-overlapping sectors** (S0–S10, §2). Every path in the repo has exactly one sector owner (CODEOWNERS, App. C).
2. Each sector has **task cards** (§4), 118 (Delivery A and B), each with PRD refs, root cause, files, tests, verification and Definition of Done.
3. A team **holds a sector by lock** (`scripts/r1-lock.mjs`) and **claims a card by lock** (R1-START-HERE, decision 1); the issue is the board. One claimant per card; the team holding the sector lock is the sector lead.
4. Work is delivered in **2-week sprints** (§5A) whose exit is measured by **PRD acceptance criteria passing in a visible browser on UAT (a clone of V2 production data)**, proven in CI first and then on UAT by the card's team (two-stage, D113); the comparison with V2 is the Delivery Lead's, not the card team's. Sprint exit is measured by ACs passing, not by tasks closed.
5. Nothing on V1, V2 or UAT is changed without the user's explicit approval (§7). UAT writes are run only by the designated UAT developer (D95 and D110: no AI session acts on a server).
6. **Acceptance is on UAT only (D110).** There is no local acceptance environment. Developers run unit/integration tests and CI (lint, typecheck, test) locally and in CI; functional acceptance evidence (card ACs, AC proofs, AC-CHAIN-1..10) comes from UAT (`uat.prestasiplus.cloud`, a clone of V2 production data) in a visible browser at 375/768/1280.
7. **Content work belongs to ADMIN users (D110).** Mapping questions to the right Bab, supplying missing figure images for "Rajah n" questions and regrouping stimulus groups are done by Admin users through the Portal Pentadbiran (improved question entry, "Soalan tanpa Bab" work-list, diagram audit, regroup work-list). Developers build and fix those screens; they do not do the content work.

**Where work happens.** Each session has its own clone or worktree; two sessions never share a checked-out branch. Screenshots and logs go in `.evidence/<task-id>/` (gitignored). UAT holds real pupil data, so evidence taken on UAT stays local and is never committed; issues and PRs carry pass/fail and IDs only. V2/V1 captures are never committed anywhere; they exist only on the Delivery Lead's PC.

---

## 0B. Improve, not rebuild

This plan closes the gaps of the existing V3; it is not a redevelopment. This is **not a rewrite.** All work is done on the existing V3 codebase (branch `feature/v3-bijak-r1`: Vue 3 + Nuxt UI in `src/`, NestJS + Prisma in `apps/api/`, `packages/contract`, `packages/exam-core`). The routes, menus, modules, API endpoints, contract, database schema and seeds stay; each card starts from a named existing file, fixes what is wrong, completes what is half-built, and builds only what does not exist. No framework, folder layout or data model is replaced.

Every card carries one **Type**, chosen after checking the code:
- **Fix**: the code exists but is wrong (a wrong route or redirect, a hidden menu, a bad rule, a bug).
- **Complete**: partly built (a backend with no screen, a screen with no backend, missing states or fields).
- **Build**: nothing exists yet.
- **Verify**: it already works; the card proves the ACs and closes small gaps.
- **Ops**: data, environment or governance work, not product code.

| Type | Delivery A cards | Delivery B cards | Total cards | Estimate (ideal days; S=1, M=3, L=6) |
|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| Fix | 25 | 0 | 25 | 64 |
| Complete | 39 | 4 | 43 | 178 |
| Build | 31 | 5 | 36 | 144 |
| Verify | 2 | 0 | 2 | 9 |
| Ops | 12 | 0 | 12 | 28 |
| **Total** | **109** | **9** | **118** | **423** |

Three cards that were typed Verify need product code, so they are re-typed (F-22, D113): S5-T10 Fix, S7-T01 Complete, S10-T07 Complete (the table above already counts them). S6-T06 is re-typed Fix to Complete in round 4 (it owns the whole of R1-GUR-44: CSV import, conditional DSKP form, the six-tile Alat Soalan block, stats and badges). The totals are recomputed from the cards by the Delivery Lead whenever cards are added or split (for example S1-T09a/S1-T09b).

**Mandatory card sections (D115).** Every card in section 4 lists four bullets after "Starts from (existing)": **Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild)**, **Change**, **Remove**, **Build (absent in V3)**, each with `path:line` from the code. A PR that adds a new file duplicating an existing V3 component, route, service or table fails review (the reviewer checks the card's "Exists in V3" bullet first). A card is written from the docs alone: a developer has no browser, no V1 and no V2. If a detail is missing from the card or the PRD, stop and report it as a documentation defect; do not guess.

**UI rule (product owner, 2026-11-04, D115).** "Jangan sampai UI V3 = V2. Kita nak betulkan functional je. UI dan layout V3 better." V2 and V1 are references ONLY for functions, data, business rules, flows, states, validations and messages. The existing V3 UI, layout, components, navigation structure and visual design STAY. Never restyle or re-lay-out a V3 screen to look like V2, and never add a V2-only visual element (banner carousel, mascot, V2 widget layout). Where a PRD screen spec was written "from V2", it defines fields, actions, rules, copy and states, not layout; V3's existing layout wins.

Only **Build** is new code; Fix, Complete and Verify reuse what exists. (Estimates are rough and are re-baselined after Sprint 1.)

**Card ownership rules (D113-continued, M-8; the same text is R1-START-HERE decision 12).** (a) A card owns EVERY FR and AC of the PRD modules on its `PRD:` line, including IDs added to those modules later, unless another card names that ID explicitly on its own line (explicit beats module). (b) `Starts from (existing)` lists the entry files. The card may also edit any other EXISTING file of ITS OWN sector that its gap check shows is on the path of one of its FR/AC (the gap-check row names the file; the scope guard only warns when no Starts-from file is touched and FAILs on another sector's path); a file of another sector needs a cross-sector PR (R1-START-HERE decision 7). (c) A card creates a new file only where its `New files:` line says; a card without that line creates none. A test file for the card's own code may be created beside that code. A card may also create the migration file(s) that a DM row it owns names, with no `New files:` entry needed; a migration on a table whose schema file belongs to another sector is written by the holder of that sector at the card's request (decision 7), and if no team holds that sector the requesting card's team writes it in a PR labelled `needs:S<n>` that the product owner reviews. (b) applies to every card Type except Keep, Verify and Ops: a Build card too may edit the existing files of its own sector that its gap check shows are on the path (route, menu and module wiring included), and creates new files only at its `New files:` paths (round 4). (d) Parts (round 4): a big card may be delivered in several PRs that all name the same card ID ("parts"); each PR states which FR/AC it proves; the card is Done when all its ACs pass. (e) A PRD module on the `PRD:` line of several cards is either split by ID in the table of section 4.0 E (then each card owns only the IDs listed there for it) or layered (a backend card and a screen card of the same module: each proves the ACs through its own layer and the screen card writes the UAT ledger row).

**Five concrete examples of reuse**
1. **Prestasi 360 exists but a redirect hides it.** `Prestasi360Page.vue` and the `/prestasi/:userId` route are built; `routes/prestasi.ts` sends the pupil to the school dashboard and lists `student` in `PRESTASI_SCHOOL_ROLES`. S10-T01 is a Fix, not a new screen.
2. **Two paper editors exist in V3 today; the target is one (the merge).** `PaperEditorPage.vue` (route `/admin/spm/kertas/:id`, with list `/admin/spm/kertas` and `/admin/spm/kertas/baharu`; `src/modules/m28-bijak-spm/routes/admin-papers.ts:61-81`) and the Set Builder (`SetBuilderLandingPage.vue` at `/admin/studio/sets` and `SetBuilderPage.vue` at `/admin/studio/sets/:paper/builder`; `routes/admin-builder.ts:20,30`) both exist, and the menu lists both ("Pembina Kertas" KS-09 and "Set Builder" KS-17, `src/shells/pentadbiran/menu.ts:123-124`). There are **no redirects yet**. S3-T02 and S2-T03 do the merge on the existing files (Set Builder stays as the single builder; `/admin/spm/kertas/:id` stays as "Semak Kertas Simulasi"); the redirects are added by those cards, not assumed.
3. **The gamification and shop backends exist, so only admin screens are needed.** `apps/api/src/modules/gamification/admin.controller.ts` and `shop/` already serve the data; S7-T04/T05 add the Pentadbiran pages and fix the pupil side against them.
4. **Headcount, Analisa Item and Laporan Soalan are built but hidden.** For the pupil, `STUDENT_HIDDEN_IDS` in `src/shells/nav/user-nav.ts:749-753` holds exactly three ids: `analisa-item`, `headcount`, `laporan-mingguan`. "Laporan Soalan" is hidden differently: its route `/laporan-soalan` is absent from the pupil's allowed list in `src/app/prototype-scope.ts`. S0-T05/T06 un-hide them and S10-T02/T03 and S5-T06 fix what is wrong inside.
5. **Tracking A has a backend with no screen.** `apps/api/src/modules/spm/tracking.service.ts` exists while `/admin/spm/tracking-a` is a 404; S3-T05 is a Complete (add the lane list and set editor on the existing service). Likewise the DSKP loader (`docs/ops/dskp-loader.md`) already exists: S4-T02 is a Verify plus an Ops run, not new code (it changes no product code; a failing AC there becomes a separate Fix card).


### Guardrails: analyse, find the gap, close it, never rebuild (mandatory, user instruction 2026-11-04)

**1. Mandatory per-card workflow (in this order; a developer or a cloud agent skips none).**
1. **Code gap check before any code.** For every FR and AC on the card, record in the existing V3 code whether it **works / is broken / is missing**, with `file:line` evidence, and post it as the issue's **"Gap check"** section (table: FR/AC, status, evidence, planned change). **A card cannot move to In progress without it.** The "Gap:" line on the card is a starting hypothesis, not the evidence.
2. **Confirm scope.** If the code has changed since the PRD's "Current gap" was written, update the Gap check, not the PRD intent. The intent (FR/AC) never changes in a card.
3. **Close the gaps only**, in the existing files named under "Starts from (existing)".
4. **Prove, in two steps (D113).** (a) *Before the PR, in CI:* tag every AC of the card `[CI]` (proven by a named automated test: unit, integration or e2e on the CI seed; UI also at 375, 768 and 1280 px with no horizontal scroll), `[UAT]` (needs UAT data or a human look) or `[CI+UAT]`. (b) *After the merge,* when the designated UAT developer has deployed `feature/v3-bijak-r1` to UAT (daily; the commit is posted in the pinned issue #503), the card's team runs the `[UAT]` ACs and every AC-CHAIN-n that passes through the card (40 section X9, card S9-T06) in a **visible browser at 375, 768 and 1280 px** with the team's shared test accounts (D110: no local acceptance) and writes Pass/Fail rows in `docs/plan/r1-ac-ledger.md`. A Fail re-opens the card. Evidence in `.evidence/<card>/` (gitignored, never committed; only pass/fail and IDs go into issues, PRs and the ledger).

**2. Change budget by card Type.**

| Type | Allowed in the diff |
|---|---|
| **Keep** | No product-code change; ACs are proven only. |
| **Verify** | No product code; the card proves ACs only. A card that turns out to need product code is re-typed Fix or Complete by the team lead in the issue before any code (the scope guard FAILs a Verify PR that changes `src/`, `apps/` or `packages/`). |
| **Ops** | No product code (data, environment, governance, scripts under `apps/api/scripts/uat-ops/` only when the card names them). |
| **Fix**, **Complete** | Edit the files under "Starts from (existing)" (and the files the Gap check names there); a **new file only when the card names it**. |
| **Build** | New files **only at the location the card names**; reuse the existing services, components and tables around it. |

**3. Prohibited (a PR containing any of these is rejected).**
- creating a **parallel** page, service, store, route or table for something that already exists;
- deleting or rewriting a working page or module;
- a schema change without a **DM ID** (listed on the card and in 50);
- an endpoint without an **API ID** (listed on the card and in 60).

**4. PR template and reviewer rule.** The PR template (App. B) has these fields: card ID (exactly one), `Cross-sector:` line (or none), Gap-check link, existing files changed, the reason for each new file, DM/API IDs, the scope-guard and canonical-map results, every AC with its `[CI]`/`[UAT]` tag and the list "ACs awaiting UAT". **Reviewer rule: reject a Fix or Complete PR that does not touch its "Starts from" files or that adds a parallel implementation.** The reviewer is the team lead of the author's team, then the product owner merges (R1-START-HERE decisions 4 and 6).

**5. CI helper `scripts/r1-scope-guard.mjs` (wired by S0-T04).** Inputs: base ref, head ref and the PR body (`PR_BODY`). It reads the card ID from the PR body, looks the card up in this plan (Type, "Starts from (existing)" paths) and checks the diff:
- **FAIL** if a Fix, Complete, Keep or Verify card adds a new Vue page or route file, a NestJS controller or a Prisma model without a DM or API ID listed in the PR body;
- **FAIL** if a Keep, Verify or Ops card changes product code (`src/`, `apps/`, `packages/`) (so a card that needs code must be typed Fix or Complete first);
- **WARN** if a Fix or Complete card touches none of its "Starts from" paths;
- **WARN** if an existing file loses more than 60% of its lines.
It prints a BM and English summary; exit code 1 = FAIL, 2 = setup error, 0 = pass (warnings allowed). `node scripts/r1-scope-guard.mjs --self-test` runs its fixtures. A cross-sector small PR (R1-START-HERE decision 7) names the card it serves and carries the line `Cross-sector: S<n> (<reason>)`; the guard accepts the files of the named sector when that line is present. Plan format the script relies on (do not change it): the card line `**S<n>-T<nn> <title>** ... Type: <type>` (a split card has one lowercase letter after the number, `S1-T09a`), no heading inside a card, and "Starts from (existing)" paths in backticks, repo-relative, plain paths or directories (no `**` globs, no prose in backticks).

## 0A. Building with cloud agents (mandatory)

The system is built by **cloud agents** (one session per card). They have **no access to V1 or V2** and no server access, and never open them; the only thing they may open is UAT in a browser, with the shared test accounts their team lead holds. Everything an agent needs is in the repository. Each session has its own clone or worktree and never shares a checked-out branch with another session; a session without Docker runs unit, typecheck, lint and contract checks only and marks stack/e2e ACs "awaiting CI/UAT".

**Gap check first.** Before writing code, post the card's **Gap check** on the issue (works / broken / missing per FR and AC, with file:line, §0B workflow). The card is built from that check inside the change budget of its Type (§0B); the agent never creates a parallel implementation.

**Read order for every card, in this order:**
0. `docs/plan/R1-START-HERE.md` (the working rules) and `docs/plan/R1-FAQ.md`.
1. `docs/prd/r1/00-index.md` (IDs, conventions, "Codes in plain words") then `docs/prd/r1/40-cross-cutting.md` (reference rule, X1-X8, NFR).
2. The PRD section of each module named on the card's "PRD:" line (`10-murid.md`, `20-guru-admin-sekolah.md` or `30-admin-pusat.md`): the FRs, ACs, exact BM copy and deviations.
3. For the data and API shape: `docs/prd/r1/50-data-model-erd.md` and `docs/prd/r1/60-api-contract.md`. There are **no screenshots** in the repo: every V2/V1 screen is described in words (layout, blocks in order, exact BM copy, states) inside the SRS section of its module, so the SRS text is the only screen reference.
4. The card itself (this plan, §4, mirrored on the GitHub issue).

**Rules**
- **Agents never open V1 or V2, and never touch a server.** Using UAT in a browser with the team's shared test accounts is allowed (R1-START-HERE decision 5). If a behaviour, copy, field, state or screen is not in the PRD text, **ask your team lead**: comment `[<team>/S-<k>] QUESTION: ...` on the issue with the two places that disagree and your suggested answer, and set `status:blocked`. The team lead answers by the tie-break in R1-FAQ Q11 and records the answer on the issue. Do not guess, do not invent, do not "improve". "Not observed in V2" in the PRD is not a question: build from the FR text and the generic loading/error/empty rules (FR-X6).
- **One card per session.** Stay inside the sector's owned paths (§2); a change elsewhere is a small cross-sector PR (§3, R1-START-HERE decision 7: the card ID of the work it serves, the line `Cross-sector: S<n> (reason)`, the label `needs:S<n>`, reviewed within 1 working day by the team holding S<n>). Do not start a second card in the same session.
- **FR-X1-5, close the gaps only (user directive: "close, fix and improve the gaps only; never rebuild from scratch").** Change only what the card's gap lists. Reuse the existing routes, components, APIs and data. Never rewrite, restyle or restructure a working screen or module. A **Verify** card changes no code unless one of its ACs fails, and then fixes only that failure. Replacing a working screen, component or API needs the Delivery Lead's **written approval in the issue** before the work starts; without it the PR is rejected.
- **Banned patterns** (a PR containing any of them is rejected): placeholder strings ("akan datang", "belum tersedia", "Pratonton", prototype notices), mock or demo data in a matrix module, a new menu item or page that the PRD does not list, a renamed label, an extra feature, a feature flag that hides a matrix module (except those named in the PRD), copy in English where the PRD gives BM, a real person's name anywhere.
- **Evidence the PR must carry:** real test output (`typecheck`, `lint`, `npm test`, `be:verify`); the scope-guard and canonical-map results; the card's **AC list, every AC tagged `[CI]`, `[UAT]` or `[CI+UAT]`**, with the automated test that proves each `[CI]` AC (UI also at 375 / 768 / 1280 with no horizontal scroll); the list "ACs awaiting UAT"; the deviations from the PRD text, if any, as a numbered list. Screenshots from UAT stay in the local `.evidence/` and are never attached; the PR carries only pass/fail and IDs, never names.
- **UAT proof and Done (§6.5).** After the PR is merged and the designated UAT developer has deployed, the card's team runs the `[UAT]` ACs in a visible browser and writes the rows in `docs/plan/r1-ac-ledger.md`. A card moves to `status:done` only after UAT Pass. Comparison with V2 is the Delivery Lead's job at verification (§8); a card team never compares with V2 and never opens it.

**Using the traceability report.** `node scripts/r1-trace-check.mjs` (from the repo root) reads the PRD IDs and this plan's cards and writes `docs/plan/r1-traceability.md`: every PRD module with its FR/AC counts and the cards that cover it, and every card with the PRD modules it cites. Before starting, open that file, find your card, and read **every** PRD module listed against it. When you add or split a card, re-run the script: it must exit 0 (first line `uncovered: 0 | dangling: 0`, second line with `unknown URS: 0`, `unknown DM/API: 0` and `ownership defects: 0`). **Every card carries a line `- Data/API: DM-xx, …; API-…` (or `none`)**: the DM rows of `50-data-model-erd.md` and the `Exists-Fix`/`Missing-Build` API rows of `60-api-contract.md` that the card implements (the `Exists-OK` routes it verifies may be listed too). Each DM and each `Exists-Fix`/`Missing-Build` API is owned by **exactly one** card; a card that only reads an owned item names it in `- Also uses:`. If a DM or API has no card, add it to the most fitting card of the same sector (a new card only when nothing fits, with the card format, sector and CODEOWNERS rules, the sprint table and the totals updated). A card must name every PRD module it implements in its "PRD:" line as full IDs (`R1-MUR-17, R1-MUR-26`, `FR-MUR-QP-6`, `R1-ADM-Q-16`, `NFR-1`). CI runs the same script (S0-T04) and fails when a PRD module has no card or a card cites an unknown ID.

## 1. Goal, phases and milestones

**Goal.** Every module ticked in `docs/Matriks-Akses-Peranan .xlsx` is **fully working on real data**: no "Pratonton", "akan datang", "belum tersedia", prototype page, mock path or dead control (D100, FR-X2-0). Behaviour replicates V2 (V1 fallback). Questions are entered and served the DSKP-correct way, with stimulus groups at the centre (D90). Every screen works at 375/768/1280, question-paper screens first.

| Delivery | Scope | Exit (all must hold) |
|---|---|---|
| **Delivery A (1.0 + linked modules, D106)** | Murid, Guru, Admin Sekolah 1.0 columns + Latih Tubi (PRD 40 §X2) + every module that a 1.0 screen links to (Murid 17, 18, 20, 23, 26, 28-31, 33; Guru 2b, 8, 8b; D106) + the Admin Pusat screens that feed them (PRD 30) | Every AC of every 1.0 module passes on UAT (visible browser, side by side with V2); AC-X2-1, AC-X2-2, AC-X2-3, AC-X6-1, AC-X6-2, AC-X3-1 pass; P0 data operations done on UAT (§7); user signs off the Delivery A demo |
| **Delivery B** | Every other module ticked in the full Murid/Guru/Admin Sekolah columns (Murid A4, rows 15–40; Guru A4; AS A1–A4, 2) + the admin screens that feed them (Gamifikasi, Kedai Hadiah, Marketplace, Kawalan Reward, …). Cikgu Alesa and Keluarga Angkat stay hidden (D102) | Each module passes its ACs and is then switched on via its scope flag (FR-X2-1). No other matrix module is hidden at the end |

**Order inside Delivery A: P0 first, then P1.** P2 items (PRD priority) may slip behind the Delivery A sign-off if the user agrees, but they are not forgotten: they stay on the board.

| Milestone | When (5-dev plan, §5A) | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| M0 Foundation | end of Sprint 0 | Stack boots untouched, board live, UAT accounts and DSKP loaded |
| M1 No broken sidebar | end of Sprint 1 | AC-X2-1 passes for pupil, teacher, school admin; weekly-report, analytics, Kirkpatrick, Headcount, Prestasi, Analisa Item, Laporan Soalan work |
| M2 DSKP-correct questions | end of Sprint 2 | An admin can enter/import a paper with stimulus groups and publish it |
| M3 Bijak SPM on a phone | end of Sprint 3 | T5 pupil sits a published paper with stimulus groups on 375 px (AC-X6-2) |
| M4 Delivery A complete (RC) | end of Sprint 6 | All P0+P1 ACs pass; release checklist (§8) green |
| M5 Delivery B | Sprint 7+ | Rest of the matrix |

## 2. Sectors

Rules for all sectors: a sector owns the listed paths (read anywhere, **write only in owned paths**). A change in a forbidden path is a small PR to its owner (§3). "FE/BE/data" = skills. "Start now" = no unmet dependency.

Shorthand: `FE` = `src/`; `BE` = `apps/api/src/modules/`; `SCH` = `apps/api/prisma/schema/`; `SEED` = `apps/api/prisma/seed/`; `CT` = `packages/contract/src/`.

### S0 Platform, build, shared registries
- **Scope:** PRD 40 §X2/X5/X6 NFR-4/6/7; AC-X2-1/2/3; register 6.2b rows 3, 17, 18 (20-guru-admin-sekolah); PRD 30 §3 (admin menu), N-06 Kawalan Ciri.
- **Owned:** `.github/**`, `scripts/**`, `compose*.yaml`, `Dockerfile*`, `docs/ops/**`, `docs/plan/**`, `e2e/global/**`, `e2e/fixtures/**`, new `e2e/r1/**`; `src/app/**` (incl. `prototype-scope.ts`, `features.ts`, `router/**`), `src/shells/**` (`pentadbiran/menu.ts`, `nav/user-nav.ts`), `src/api/**`, `src/lib/**` (except `lower-form.ts`), `src/modules/m01-authentication/**`; BE `auth/`, `jobs/`, `ocr/`, `common/` (= `apps/api/src/common/**`), `index.ts`; `SCH/base.prisma`, `identity.prisma`, `etl.prisma`, `feature-flags.prisma` (new, DM-34); `SEED/index.ts`, `guard.ts`, `e2e*.ts`, `core/**`; `CT/common`, `CT/auth`, `CT/access`, `CT/session`, `CT/index.ts`, `CT/openapi.ts`, `packages/contract/openapi.json`; frozen (hidden) modules `cikgu`, `src/modules/m13-*`, `m25-*`.
- **Forbidden:** all page code of other sectors.
- **Skills:** BE/infra, small FE. **Deps:** none. **Start now: yes.**

### S1 Question model and pipeline (backend)

## 3. Shared-file protocol

| Shared file | Single owner | How others change it | Merge order |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/shells/pentadbiran/menu.ts` | S0 | Menu is changed only by S0-T07 (the target menu is FINAL, D107: no approval wait) and later by one-line cross-sector PRs to S0 (label/route/`built`; R1-START-HERE decision 7: the PR names its card, carries `Cross-sector: S0 (menu line)` and the label `needs:S0`). A sector never edits `menu.ts` in its own card PR; it makes its route exist and names the route in the cross-sector PR. | S0 first, then pages |
| `src/shells/nav/user-nav.ts` | S0 | Same. All wrong targets of the 1.0 rows are fixed in S0-T06 in one PR (so the nav is touched once). Later: PR ≤20 lines to S0, reviewed within 1 working day | S0 first |
| `src/app/prototype-scope.ts`, `features.ts`, `modules.ts`, `router/**` | S0 | **Delete-only for sectors**: when a module passes its ACs, its sector opens a PR that removes its own entry from the scope list (lines between `// [S<n> begin]` / `// [S<n> end]` markers that S0-T05 inserts). Adding an entry (hiding something) is S0 only | after the module PR |
| Route registries `src/modules/*/routes.ts`, `src/app/modules.ts` | the module's sector (routes.ts); S0 (modules.ts list) | A new module is registered by S0; routes inside a module by its sector | |
| `apps/api/prisma/schema/*.prisma` | the sector that owns that file (§2). `base.prisma` S0 | A sector needing a table/column in another sector's file asks that sector (issue label `needs:schema`); the owner adds it in **their** migration range | schema PR before consumers |
| Migrations `apps/api/prisma/migrations/` | S0 as gatekeeper of the numbering only | **Reserved ranges** below; name `0NNN_<sector>_<slug>`; one migration per PR (a card that owns several migrations, such as S7-T05 with `0223`, `0229`, `0230`, opens one PR per migration in number order; every PR body names the same card ID and the scope guard accepts that); expand-then-contract only (no destructive change in the same release); `npm run be:lint:migration` must pass | by number |
| `packages/contract/src/**`, `openapi.json` | per folder (§2); `index.ts`, `openapi.ts`, `openapi.json` S0 | see contract rules | contract PR first |
| Seeds `apps/api/prisma/seed/**` | per file (§2); `index.ts`, `guard.ts`, `e2e*.ts`, `core/**` S0 | A sector never edits another sector's seed; asks for a fixture by issue. New fixtures go in the sector's own file and are registered in `index.ts` by S0 in the same PR as a one-line change | seed owner first |
| `package.json`, `package-lock.json` | S0 | New dependency = PR to S0 with reason (no new dependency without it) | |
| `docs/TRACKER.md` | Delivery Lead | Sectors do not edit it | |

Prisma applies pending migrations by name, so a lower number landing after a higher one is safe if the tables are independent. A migration that depends on another sector's migration states it in the PR and waits for it to merge. Data repairs for UAT are **scripts** (`apps/api/scripts/uat-ops/`), run by the designated UAT developer, never migrations (as `dskp-loader.md` does). **Exception (D113-continued, M-9):** a data migration is allowed only when it ships in the same PR as the schema change it completes and is idempotent (re-runnable; dry-run first on UAT by the designated UAT developer). Migration `0229_s7_reward_catalogue_merge` (DM-77) qualifies; DM-77 names the U-OP that runs its dry run.

**Contract-change rules.**
1. Additive only inside Release 1 (new optional field, new endpoint, new enum value with a safe default). A breaking change needs an ADR and S0's approval.
2. The contract change is its **own small PR** (`CT/<folder>` + `npm run contract:emit` + regenerated `openapi.json`), merged **before** the BE and FE PRs that use it. Run `npm run contract:check` and `npm run be:verify`.
3. `openapi.json` is generated: on a merge conflict, take either side and re-run `npm run contract:emit`; never hand-merge.
4. FE uses only the contract type; no ad-hoc response shapes.
5. A change in a folder owned by another sector needs that sector's review (CODEOWNERS enforces it).

**Rebase and merge rules.** Short-lived branches from `feature/v3-bijak-r1`; rebase onto it daily; **squash merge**; CI green is required; prefer PRs of ≤400 changed lines. Order in a day: S0 → contract PRs → schema/migration PRs → BE → FE. A PR waiting more than 1 working day for a cross-sector review is escalated to the Delivery Lead (S9), who may reassign the review. No long-lived integration branches (the old `team/*`, `be/integration-*` pattern is not used).

**V-STD (verification, all cards unless the card says otherwise).**
1. Before merge (developer): unit/integration tests and CI (`typecheck`, `lint`, `npm test`, `be:verify`) locally and in CI. The local stack and the demo/e2e seed are for these automated tests only; they are not acceptance evidence (D110).
2. After the change is deployed to UAT (`uat.prestasiplus.cloud`, a clone of V2 production data): run the card's ACs in a **visible (headed) browser** (Playwright MCP or manual; never headless) at **375, 768 and 1280 px**, logged in as the role(s) named on the card; check `document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth` (AC-X6-1); save screenshots to `.evidence/<task-id>/<width>-<screen>.png` (gitignored; UAT holds real pupil data, so they are never committed and issues/PRs carry only pass/fail and IDs, never names).
3. **V2 comparison.** The Delivery Lead compares with V2 at verification; a card team never opens V2 and lists deviations from the PRD text in its PR.
4. **Data:** step 2 already runs on UAT data; if a data operation (U-OP-nn, §7) the card depends on is not yet done on UAT, say so in the issue and re-run the AC after that step.
5. No text from the placeholder list (§8, AC-X2-3) on any screen touched.

**DoD-T (task Definition of Done).** (a) every AC on the card passes with evidence; (b) `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint`, `npm test`, and for BE `npm run be:verify` green (real output pasted); (c) tests added as listed; (d) every AC is tagged `[CI]`, `[UAT]` or `[CI+UAT]`, and `[CI]` ACs are proven by named automated tests; the V-STD result on UAT is recorded as pass/fail and AC IDs in the ledger and the PR (screenshots stay in the gitignored `.evidence/`; no names; D110); (e) no new placeholder string, no new mock path in the http build; (f) PRD/doc updated if behaviour diverges; (f2) **FR-X1-5:** no rewrite of working code; only the gaps listed on the card changed, existing files reused (listed in the PR), and any replacement of a working screen, component or API has the Delivery Lead's written approval in the issue; (f3) the issue carries the Gap check section, the PR names exactly one card and lists its DM/API IDs, and `scripts/r1-scope-guard.mjs` has no FAIL (§0B); (g) PR reviewed by the author's team lead; the team holding any other sector touched approved (label `needs:S<n>`); the product owner merged; (h) after the designated UAT developer's deploy, the `[UAT]` ACs have Pass rows in the ledger; the team lead set `status:done`.

**Card Type** (D-39): where the Action of a PRD module differs from the Type of a card that carries it, the card Type governs (it is the higher of its modules).

**Sizes** (ideal focused days for one developer): S = 1, M = 3, L = 6. Anything >6 must be split before it is claimed.

Priority: P0 blocks 1.0; P1 important; P2 polish. Delivery: A unless stated.

**S0-T02 Stack boots with no manual step** · P0 · M · Type: Fix · deps: none
- Starts from (existing): `apps/api/prisma/seed/e2e.ts` (and `e2e-k2.ts`, `e2e-trial.ts`, `exam-launch-blueprints.ts` in the same folder), `compose.yaml`, `compose.http.yaml`, `.github/workflows/e2e-http.yml`, `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): compose waits for migrate (`compose.yaml:51-55`); `compose.http.yaml:22-42` already runs `be:db:migrate`, `be:db:seed`, then `NODE_ENV=development E2E_SEED=1 E2E_DEMO_SECONDARY=1 be:db:seed -- --profile=e2e`; `npm run stack:up` (`package.json:40`); the CI job in `.github/workflows/e2e-http.yml:79-80` already runs `stack:up`; the e2e seed already reviews layer-2 values in place before checking (`apps/api/prisma/seed/e2e.ts:124-131`) and prints the codes from `validateForVerification` at `e2e.ts:152`.
- Change: FIRST reproduce on a clean clone and record the real failing step in the PR. The quoted `NO_QUOTA`/`NO_KBAT` failure is not reproducible by reading the code (those words appear only in a spec and the contract), so if nothing fails the card reduces to the Build line below plus closing the stale gap text in `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md`. If a step fails, fix that step in `e2e.ts` / `compose.http.yaml` (S3 reviews a blueprint-file edit). Keep the default blueprint `3472/2` (the only one `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md` says passes as seeded).
- Build (absent in V3): the smoke check `stack-smoke`, delivered as ONE new workflow step named `stack-smoke` inside the existing single job of `.github/workflows/e2e-http.yml` (not `frontend.yml`, where the stack is never started; not a second job, so the stack boots once). The step goes after `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium` and before the step `E2E (real API)` (`npm run test:e2e:http`), and runs `npx playwright test -c playwright.http.config.ts e2e/api/stack-smoke.spec.ts`. New spec `e2e/api/stack-smoke.spec.ts` (tag `@api`, imports `test, expect` from `../fixtures/api`, no UI): for each of the five accounts of `e2e/api/helpers.ts` `account(role)` it calls `page.request.post('/api/auth/login', {data: {identifier: user, password: pass}})` and expects status 200, then `page.request.get('/api/session')` and expects status 200 and `active.role` equal to: `student` -> `student`, `studentB` -> `student`, `guru` -> `teacher`, `adminSekolah` -> `school_admin`, `adminPusat` -> `admin`. The password is whatever `account(role)` returns (it already resolves `BIJAK_<ROLE>_PASS`, then `E2E_DEMO_PASSWORD`, then the fixture constant `API_PASSWORD` = `Demo1234`); the spec never reads a password itself. The spec also matches `playwright.http.config.ts` `testMatch` `**/api/**/*.spec.ts`, so it runs again in the main run (cheap, harmless).
- PRD: FR-X5-2. Gap: e2e seed fails `blueprint 3472/2 does not pass the Sahkan checks (NO_QUOTA, NO_KBAT)` and the API does not start by itself (gap §6, `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md`).
- Data/API: DM-21; API-AUTH-17
- Also uses: DM-39 (owned by another card; read or verified here, not an ownership claim)
- Approach: reproduce on a clean clone; fix the e2e fixture so the verified blueprint has quota and KBAT (S3 reviews the blueprint file edit) or make the seed use the Disahkan fixture path; make `compose.http.yaml` wait for migrate+seed; document one command. Add the `stack-smoke` step (above) that logs in as the 5 roles.
- Files: `apps/api/prisma/seed/e2e.ts`, `e2e-k2.ts`, `e2e-trial.ts` (S3-owned, one-off edit with S3 review), `exam-launch-blueprints.ts`, `compose.yaml`, `compose.http.yaml`, `.github/workflows/e2e-http.yml`, `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md`, new `e2e/api/stack-smoke.spec.ts`.
- API/contract/migration: none.
- Tests: CI step `stack-smoke` (`e2e/api/stack-smoke.spec.ts`). Verify: fresh clone, `npm ci`, `npm run stack:up`, open http://localhost:5192 (the compose default `FRONTEND_PORT`, `compose.yaml:26`; 5193 exists nowhere), log in as the five `account(role)` users: Admin Pusat, Guru, Admin Sekolah, the e2e pupil (Tingkatan 4, `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md`) and `studentB`. Decided (D115): the check is for T4 only; there is no named T1 pupil in the seeds, so "murid T1" is dropped from Verify (T1 data is covered by S4/S5 cards).
- DoD: DoD-T + a second session (another developer, or a cloud agent session with Docker) reproduces it on a clean clone and pastes the command output in the PR; a cloud session without Docker marks this DoD item "awaiting CI" and the green CI step `stack-smoke` is the proof. If the clean-clone run shows that nothing fails, the PR is allowed to contain only the `stack-smoke` step + spec and the `docs/ops/e2e-seed.md` edit.

- Starts from (existing): `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/ocr-engine.ts` (`class TesseractEngine`, line 65, local `langPath`, no CDN; `createOcrEngine`, line 162), `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/pdfjs.engine.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/ocr.config.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/jobs/jobs.worker.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/jobs/jobs.config.ts`, `apps/api/src/common/health/ready.controller.ts` (the existing `GET /api/ready`, API-AUTH-17: it gains one additive field `worker`, one of `ok`, `down` or `disabled`; no new route), `docker/api/Dockerfile`, `.env.api.example`, `deploy/vps/api.env.example`, `deploy/vps/compose.prod.yaml`.

- Change: (1) `ocr.config.ts:41` default of `OCR_PAGE_TIMEOUT_MS` 30000 -> 90000 (this card owns `ocr/**`; S1-T04 only reads it; `env.schema.ts:179` has no default, keep it optional). (2) `jobs.worker.ts` `tick()`: stamp `lastTickAt` at the START of `tick()`, before any early return (a long `dispatch` or `isDraining` must not look like a dead worker); with the 1 s `JOBS_POLL_MS` the stamp is at most about 1 s apart, well inside the 15 s tick-cadence rule and the 60 s `ok` window of the Worker field below (two different numbers for two different things: cadence at most 15 s, `ok` window 60 s). (3) `.env.api.example:128,133` (`JOBS_WORKER_ENABLED=false`, `OCR_ENGINE=none`) and `deploy/vps/api.env.example:46` (`OCR_ENGINE=none`; production compose already sets the worker true, `deploy/vps/compose.prod.yaml:78`) -> worker on, `OCR_ENGINE=tesseract`, `OCR_LANG_DIR=/opt/tessdata`; in `.env.api.example` also change the printed default `OCR_PAGE_TIMEOUT_MS=30000` to `90000`; in `deploy/vps/api.env.example` replace the comment "DECIDED (AZ-29 item 4): OFF at go-live ... Do not set OCR_LANG_DIR" with a line saying D112 supersedes it (real OCR in-house, `OCR_ENGINE=tesseract`, `OCR_LANG_DIR=/opt/tessdata`; real run on UAT only after U-OP-02). Compose needs no edit: `compose.yaml` (`api` service, line 42-43) and `compose.http.yaml` load `.env.api`, which `stack:up` and CI copy from `.env.api.example`, so the example is the one place that sets `JOBS_WORKER_ENABLED=true` and `OCR_LANG_DIR=/opt/tessdata` for the `api` service (D115 default).
- Build (absent in V3): `apps/api/src/modules/jobs/jobs.health.ts` (`getWorkerHealth()`) and the `worker` field in `ready.controller.ts`; a tessdata stage in `docker/api/Dockerfile` (none today). The runtime image is alpine (stage at line 33) and the container is `read_only` with a tmpfs `/tmp` (`compose.yaml:59-62`), so `/opt/tessdata` must be baked into the image, never downloaded at run time. Download URLs (D115 default, verify when building): `https://github.com/tesseract-ocr/tessdata_fast/raw/4.1.0/msa.traineddata` and `https://github.com/tesseract-ocr/tessdata_fast/raw/4.1.0/eng.traineddata`. The developer downloads both files ONCE on a machine with access to github.com, computes `sha256sum` and writes the two values as the `ARG` defaults `TESSDATA_MSA_SHA256` and `TESSDATA_ENG_SHA256`; a session with no outbound access to github.com cannot do this step and writes in the PR "Blocked: needs the two SHA-256 values from a session with network access" (a teammate or the Delivery Lead pastes them; the rest of the card is not blocked).
- Decided (D115): do NOT create `ocr/tesseract.engine.ts` (it would duplicate `TesseractEngine`); the unit test with a stubbed recogniser targets the existing class: first look in `apps/api/test` for an existing OCR engine spec and extend it.
- New files: `apps/api/src/modules/jobs/jobs.health.ts` (the worker-alive check read by `ready.controller.ts`). No new OCR engine file and no new dependency.
- PRD: Q-07 FR-ADM-Q07.5, QE-05 FR-ADM-QE5.7, Q-18 FR-ADM-Q18.5; gap F2. D112(2): extraction is REAL OCR, in-house (Tesseract worker, `OCR_ENGINE=tesseract`); there is NO external AI provider in R1 (ALESA/AI stays hidden, D73/D102; pupil data stays private); extraction is rule-based and an admin always reviews before anything publishes; a mock OCR exists only in CI tests and is never configured on UAT. Gap: extraction stuck at "Sedang diproses" because `JOBS_WORKER_ENABLED=false`, `OCR_ENGINE=none` (`apps/api/src/common/config/env.schema.ts:169,175`; `jobs/jobs.worker.ts`, `intake/files/intake-processing.service.ts`).
- Data/API: none
- Also uses: API-AUTH-17 (owned by S0-T02; read or verified here, not an ownership claim; this card adds only the `worker` field)
- Worker field (D113-continued): `jobs.health.ts` exports `getWorkerHealth()`: `disabled` when `JOBS_WORKER_ENABLED` is false; otherwise `ok` when `lastTickAt` is at most 60 s old, else `down`. The jobs worker loop stamps `lastTickAt` (a module variable in `jobs/jobs.health.ts`, no table) on every tick, first line of `tick()` (the same rule as 60 API-AUTH-17: ticks at most 15 s apart even when no job is queued; the `ok` window is 60 s); `down` also when the worker never ticked since start. `ready.controller.ts` adds `worker` beside its existing fields; the field is informational only and the HTTP status does NOT change because of it.
- Language data (D113-continued): the API image never downloads language data at run time (`tesseract.js` runs with `langPath = OCR_LANG_DIR`, no CDN). The API `Dockerfile` (`docker/api/Dockerfile`) gets one build stage that fetches `msa.traineddata` and `eng.traineddata` from the pinned `tessdata_fast` 4.1.0 release of the Tesseract project, checks the SHA-256 written in the Dockerfile (the developer computes the two values once from the downloaded pinned files and writes them as the build arguments `TESSDATA_MSA_SHA256` and `TESSDATA_ENG_SHA256`; the build fails on a mismatch) and copies them to `/opt/tessdata`; compose and the UAT env template set `OCR_ENGINE=tesseract` and `OCR_LANG_DIR=/opt/tessdata`. No data file is committed to the repo. CI builds the same image; `docs/ops/api-runbook.md` documents it.
- CI proof: the e2e uses a text-layer fixture PDF (needs no OCR) and a unit test of `TesseractEngine` (`ocr-engine.ts`) with a stubbed recogniser; real Tesseract runs on UAT only, after U-OP-02.
- Approach: enable worker and `OCR_ENGINE=tesseract` (+ the traineddata folder above) in compose and in the UAT env template; health endpoint reports worker state; document UAT enablement as U-OP-02 (OPS, user approval, §7). The in-service timeout/error state is S1-T04.
- Files: `compose*.yaml`, API `Dockerfile`, `docs/ops/api-runbook.md`, `apps/api/src/common/config/env.schema.ts`, `apps/api/src/common/health/ready.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/jobs/**`, `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/**`.
- Tests: jobs worker unit test; e2e: upload a small PDF, batch leaves "Sedang diproses".
- Verify: V-STD steps 1, 2 on `/admin/spm/soalan/muat-naik`.
- DoD: DoD-T.

- Starts from (existing): `scripts/lint.mjs`, `.github/workflows/frontend.yml` (job `verify`, steps from line 42); `scripts/r1-trace-check.mjs`, `scripts/r1-scope-guard.mjs`, `.github/workflows/r1-docs.yml`, `.github/workflows/r1-scope-guard.yml` (present on `feature/v3-bijak-r1` only after PR #406).
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): `scripts/lint.mjs` and the `verify` job of `.github/workflows/frontend.yml`. `scripts/r1-*.mjs`, `.github/workflows/r1-*.yml`, `.github/CODEOWNERS`, `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/r1-card.yml`, `docs/prd` and `docs/plan` exist today only on branch `docs/r1-prd-devplan`; they reach `feature/v3-bijak-r1` only when PR #406 merges. `check-placeholders.mjs` and the allow-list do not exist anywhere yet.

- Build (absent in V3): `scripts/check-placeholders.mjs`, `scripts/placeholder-allowlist.json`, their self-test.
- Sub-steps (the paragraph below is the specification; do them in this order): A script + self-test; B allow-list JSON as a mirror of table X2-3-A (rows P-01..P-07, D-01..D-13); C wire into `scripts/lint.mjs` in `report` mode; D required checks (owner action by the product owner); E part 2: flip the default to `fail` as the first S0 PR of Sprint 4.

Do not copy the list into code by hand: the script reads it from that sentence.

- New files: `scripts/check-placeholders.mjs`, `scripts/placeholder-allowlist.json`. No new workflow file: `.github/workflows/r1-scope-guard.yml` (workflow `r1-scope-guard`, job `scope-guard`, which also runs the canonical-map check) and `.github/workflows/r1-docs.yml` (the trace check) already exist; this card extends them and sets them as required checks, so until this card merges every team runs the scripts locally (R1-START-HERE step 6).
- PRD: AC-X2-3, FR-X2-0, FR-ADM-H0.2 (the no-placeholder rule of 30). **Also owns the traceability gate:** add `node scripts/r1-trace-check.mjs` (exists; the lead owns its logic, S0 only wires it) as a required CI step in `.github/workflows/frontend.yml`. It regenerates `docs/plan/r1-traceability.md` and fails unless it exits 0 (`uncovered: 0 | dangling: 0`, no unknown id, no DM/API ownership defect) (a PRD module without a card, or a card citing an unknown PRD ID). Approach for the placeholder check: `scripts/check-placeholders.mjs` fails the build if module code (`src/modules/**` and `src/shells/**`, excluding tests and the allow-list) contains any banned string of the ONE list written in 40 AC-X2-3 (master). This card, R1-START-HERE, the FAQ answer on placeholders, 10 section 4.11 and the register table C cite that list and never restate or extend it; the script reads the strings from the list as written in 40 AC-X2-3 and its self-test fails when the script and that list differ. The banned-string rule (D113-continued, L-13) is about placeholder PAGES and notices; a real admin function whose label contains a banned word is not a placeholder. Allow-list `scripts/placeholder-allowlist.json` (P-1, run 5): the file is the mirror of table X2-3-A of 40 AC-X2-3 (rows P-01 to P-07 the admin preview labels, rows D-01 to D-13 the kept data-empty and defensive strings such as "Peta Belum Tersedia", "Bank soalan belum sedia", "Kedudukan belum tersedia" and the three defensive pupil strings of 10 section 4.11 #9-#11, and the officers page of register row 18). Its shape is `entries[]` with the keys `id`, `string`, `screens` (the repository path glob of each screen, added by this card), `condition` and `prd`; the self-test parses table X2-3-A (rows whose first cell is a P-nn or D-nn id) and the JSON and fails on any id or string present in one and missing in the other, and it extracts the banned strings from the sentence of AC-X2-3 that starts "contains, compared without regard to letter case, any banned string:" (every double-quoted string up to the next full stop) and compares them with the strings the script uses. Anywhere else a banned string fails the check. A further real admin function is added by a one-line PR to `scripts/placeholder-allowlist.json` naming the FR and the path glob, together with the same line in the 40 AC-X2-3 allow-list. Wire into `npm run lint` (`package.json` runs `node scripts/lint.mjs`, which calls `scripts/check-placeholders.mjs` as its last step). Mode switch (run 6): the script reads `PLACEHOLDER_CHECK_MODE` (`report` or `fail`); its default in the code, in the local `npm run lint` and in `.github/workflows/frontend.yml` is `report` until S0-T04 part 2 (start of Sprint 4) changes the default to `fail` in one PR (the strings are removed by the cards of Sprints 1 to 3). In Sprints 1 to 3 the Verify step reads the report output. **This card owns the Sprint 4 flip (P-1, run 5):** its second PR, titled `[team/S-k] S0-T04 part 2 (placeholder check to fail)` and opened as the first S0 PR of Sprint 4 (the card is listed in the Sprint 4 cards of 5A), changes the default to `fail` in that one PR (script default, local lint and workflow) and adds a fixture file with a banned string that turns the build red; the AC of part 2 is "[CI] with `PLACEHOLDER_CHECK_MODE=fail` the fixture exits 1 and the real tree exits 0". The card is Done after part 2 merges. The check is wired in `scripts/lint.mjs` (inside `npm run lint`, so every workflow that runs lint runs it; no separate workflow step). **Also wires the scope guard (§0B, guardrail 5):** make the existing job `scope-guard` of `.github/workflows/r1-scope-guard.yml` a REQUIRED status check (it checks out with `fetch-depth: 0` and runs `node scripts/r1-scope-guard.mjs --base origin/<target branch> --head HEAD` with `env: PR_BODY: ${{ github.event.pull_request.body }}`); exit code 1 (FAIL) blocks the merge, 2 (setup error, e.g. no card ID in the PR body) also blocks, warnings are printed in the job summary. The script exists (`--self-test` runs its fixtures); S0 wires it and does not change its rules without the Delivery Lead. Making a job a required check is a branch-protection setting that only the product owner (repository admin) can change: this card's PR body lists the check names (`scope-guard` and `r1-docs`) and the card is Done when the product owner confirms in the PR that both are required. The trace check already runs in `r1-docs.yml`; `frontend.yml` gets the step only if `r1-docs.yml` does not run on every PR (one run per PR, never twice).

CI and local lint use "report" until the end of Sprint 3 and "fail" from Sprint 4 (mode switch above).

**S0-T08 Rate limits and 429 UX** · P1 · S · Type: Fix · deps: none
- Starts from (existing): `apps/api/src/common/security/global-rate-limit.guard.ts`, `apps/api/src/common/throttle/rate-limit.ts`, `apps/api/src/common/config/env.schema.ts`, `compose.http.yaml`, `src/api/http-transport.ts`, `src/api/errors.ts`, `src/ds/DsLoadError.vue`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): defaults are already high: `THROTTLE_ADMIN_READ_PER_MINUTE` 300 (`env.schema.ts:46`, `users.constants.ts:42`), the global per-IP ceiling `THROTTLE_GLOBAL_PER_MINUTE` default 300 (`global-rate-limit.guard.ts:14-18`, per client address), curriculum admin write bucket 30/min per user (`curriculum.constants.ts:12`), Set Builder read 240 (`builder.constants.ts:11`). On the client: `ApiError` carries `retryAfter` (`src/api/errors.ts:60,74`) and maps 429 to `RATE_LIMITED` (line 48); the API sets `Retry-After` on some 429s (`builder.service.ts:666`); `DsLoadError.vue` (87 lines) has "Cuba Semula".
- Change: FIRST reproduce (20 admin pages at about 2 s each) and record which bucket fires (the 429 body and headers). The likely cause is the shared per-IP global bucket (all users behind one school proxy share 300/min) plus write buckets of 30, not the admin-read bucket. `src/api/http-transport.ts` never reads the `Retry-After` header: read it into `ApiError.retryAfter` when the body has none. `DsLoadError.vue` gets a 429 case with the ERR-RATE-01 copy of 40 X10.11: "Terlalu banyak percubaan. Sila cuba sebentar lagi." plus "Cuba lagi dalam {s} saat." with the existing `Cuba Semula` button enabled after the countdown; never an empty "Ralat". S0-T08 fixes only the buckets and this 429 message; the automatic retry policy (GET retried when `retryAfter` is at most 10) is S0-T12's (40 X10.3.4).
- Decided (D115): for signed-in requests the global bucket is keyed per user (`per: 'user'`) at 1200 per minute (D115 default); the per-IP bucket of 300 stays for anonymous requests and the login routes stay strict (ERR-AUTH-09 limits unchanged). Keep admin read at 300 unless the reproduction shows it fires. UI: keep the V3 layout and components (product-owner rule 2026-11-04); V2/V1 are references for functions, data, rules and messages only.
- PRD: NFR-4. Gap: fast admin navigation hits 429 and shows an empty "Ralat" on Kurikulum/Subjek/Tahap/Set Builder. Approach: tune throttle per route group (admin reads high, auth strict); the FE transport turns 429 into a retry message with `Retry-After`, not "Ralat".
- Data/API: none
- Files: `apps/api/src/common/**` (throttle config), `compose*.yaml` env, `src/api/**`, `src/ds/DsLoadError.vue` (S8 by request).
- Tests: BE throttle test; FE transport unit test. Verify: click 20 admin pages at ~2 s each in the visible browser: no 429.
- DoD: DoD-T.

Values (all from 40 X10.3, quoted so nobody opens a browser): client JSON timeout 30 s (`NETWORK_ERROR` subtype `TIMEOUT`, ERR-NET-02 "Permintaan mengambil masa terlalu lama. Cuba lagi."); upload: abort after 60 s without progress; export/blob and import calls 125 s; automatic retry for GET and HEAD only, on `NETWORK_ERROR`, 502, 503, 504 and 429 (429 only when `retryAfter` <= 10), at most 2 retries after 1 s and 3 s with 20 % jitter, never for POST (except Bijak autosave and submit engines); 401 -> `/login?kembali={path+query}` (same-origin only, one redirect per 2 s burst) with the banner "Sesi anda telah tamat. Sila log masuk semula."; draft kept in `sessionStorage` key `pp.draft.{routeName}.{recordId|new}` (<= 200 KB) and the notice "Terdapat kerja yang belum disimpan daripada sesi sebelum ini."; offline banner "Anda sedang luar talian. Semak sambungan internet anda." and, on recovery, "Sambungan dipulihkan." for 3 s; 5xx shows `Rujukan: {requestId}` + button `Salin`; maintenance copy (ERR-SYS-03, API-AUTH-03) "Sistem sedang diselenggara buat sementara waktu. Sila cuba semula dalam beberapa minit."; 429 copy is ERR-RATE-01 (S0-T08 builds the bucket side).
- Decided (D115): 40 X10.3.4 holds every number, so no extra default is needed. E2E spec `e2e/r1/errors.http.spec.ts` follows the wiring rule of S0-T06 (tag `@api`, import `../fixtures/api`, `playwright.http.config.ts` `testMatch` extended by S0-T06). UI: keep the V3 layout and components (product-owner rule 2026-11-04); V2/V1 are references for functions, data, rules and messages only.
- New files: `e2e/r1/errors.http.spec.ts`.
- PRD: 40 section X10 (FR-ERR-1..27, AC-ERR-1..12, the ERR catalogue X10.11 and the code changes X10.12). Gap: the API filter answers with Laravel-style English defaults and turns an unmapped 4xx into 5xx; the frontend `ApiError` has no `requestId`; no return-URL on 401, no global offline banner, no single timeout and retry policy, no double-submit lock.
- Data/API: none
- Approach: (1) envelope `{error: {code, message, fields?, details?, retryAfter?, requestId}}` with BM default messages, an unmapped 4xx kept as 4xx, and the codes `BAD_REQUEST`, `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE`, `UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE`, `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE`, `TIMEOUT`, `BAD_GATEWAY` added to the contract (X10.12 E-1, `CT/common`); (2) frontend core in `src/api/**` (E-2, E-3, E-8): `ApiError` gets `requestId` and `details`, 401 keeps the edited path and the draft (`/login?kembali=`, the banner "Sesi anda telah tamat. Sila log masuk semula.", the restore notice), a global offline banner, one client timeout and retry policy (a GET that fails with 503 is retried twice, a POST never), a double-submit lock, the toast rules, first-error focus, `Rujukan: {requestId}` with `Salin` on every 5xx; (3) error pages, the maintenance page and the chunk-load dialog use the copy of X10.3.8 (E-5, `DsErrorPage.vue`, `DsLoadError.vue`). The other X10.12 changes belong to the cards of the sector named there (E-4 and E-6 Bijak S3, E-7 and E-12 S7, E-9 S1 and S3, E-10 S1-T04, E-11 S6-T09, E-13 S2-T03). Every row of X10.11 is asserted by the card that owns the screen (AC-ERR-9); this card builds the five cross-cutting specs (AC-ERR-2..6) and the CI list of ERR ids that have no assertion.

### 4.S1 Question model and pipeline (backend)

*Principle for every S1 card:* the first step is a **characterisation test** that fails on the current behaviour named in the card (so the fix is proven, not assumed). The gap file says "root cause (file:line)"; confirm it in the first hour and note differences in the issue.

(the one catalogue text of 40; 45 section 4.2 and 60 API-Q-04 say the same) and nothing changes; a list that holds every member of a group moves the group as one unit. **DM-02(b):** the unique stimulus per group is created only when the U-OP-18 report shows 0 stimuli in more than one group (50 OD-13); migration `0120` carries only the FK change. **Canonical map:** the new stimulus-group routes (the last five API-Q rows of 60) are added to `docs/plan/r1-canonical-map.json` by `node scripts/r1-canonical-map.mjs --write` in the same PR (the PR body names the API IDs, which approves them for the scope guard).

(both gates, approve and submit; step 4; absent when the Subjek + Tingkatan has no chapter: the table is `chapters` (`apps/api/prisma/schema/curriculum.prisma:88`, `@@map("chapters")`), and "has chapters" = `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM chapters WHERE subject_id = $1 AND education_level_id = $2 AND is_active AND in_tree)`; the chosen chapter is `chapterId` of the version, already in `QuestionGateData`; the `legacy_topics` table that `learner.service.ts` reads is not used here; on a clean seed `chapters` may be empty for a level, in which case the item is simply absent). (3) Add `NO_DIFFICULTY` "Sila pilih tahap kesukaran." (step 4, the same step as Bab and Kesukaran in the Tag step of 45 section 3.1.2; `difficulty_level` is null; today only `difficultyTp` exists), migration `0128_s1_difficulty_level`. (4) Rejection: `rejected` is NOT a stored status (`QUESTION_STATUSES = draft, review, approved, archived`, `parts.ts:12`; `status.ts:7-9`): "Ditolak" means the stored status goes back to `draft` with `rejection_reason` non-null (migration 0135); `status.ts` must allow review -> draft with a reason; any other pair is ERR-CON-04 ("Perubahan status ini tidak dibenarkan daripada status semasa."). `PKSK_QUESTION_STATUSES` is a different list and is untouched. (5) `difficultyDefaulted` is a response hint added to the save result of API-Q-03 (`QuestionSaveResult` in `packages/contract/src/questions/`), never stored.

Kesukaran is set on every save (the typed value, or the default below); `NO_DIFFICULTY` fires only when `difficulty_level` is null (legacy rows); `difficultyDefaulted` (API-Q-03) is a response hint only and is never stored. The Tag step pre-fills it with `min(5, TP)`, or 3 when the question has no TP, until the admin touches it (`difficultyTouched`); a later TP change never overwrites a touched value; the server stores the typed value in `difficulty_level` (DM-47, migration `0128_s1_difficulty_level`, mapping cited in 50 section 4.2.1) and never derives it.

**S1-T04 Intake timeout, error state, manual Terima** · P0 · M · Type: Fix · deps: S0-T03
- Starts from (existing): `apps/api/src/modules/intake/files/{intake-processing.service.ts,intake-upload.service.ts,intake-files.controller.ts}`, `apps/api/src/modules/intake/workflow/{extraction.service.ts,review.service.ts,gate.ts,intake-workflow.controller.ts}`, `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/ocr.config.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/ocr/ocr.error.ts`, `packages/contract/src/intake/{files.ts,dashboard.ts,paths.ts}`, `apps/api/prisma/schema/intake.prisma`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): files controller routes `POST batches`, `GET batches`, `GET batches/:id`, `POST batches/:id/files`, `POST batches/:id/cancel`, page and figure image reads (`intake-files.controller.ts:88-162`); the batch statuses `submitted`, `processing`, `ready_for_review`, `failed`, `cancelled` (contract `files.ts:22` and `dashboard.ts:37`; DB check `intake.prisma:12`); `OCR_TIMEOUT` (`ocr.error.ts:6`); a circuit breaker for resource codes (`intake-processing.service.ts:30,246`); the manual `Terima` is `POST /api/intake/items/:id/accept` (`review.service.ts:142`; there is NO batch-level Terima).
- Change: (1) `extract` has an EMPTY body (`intake-workflow.controller.ts:64-75`, `paths.ts:186`): add the optional `pages[]` (retry of chosen pages). (2) Both status lists in the contract (`files.ts` and `dashboard.ts`) gain `selesai_dengan_ralat` together, and the DB check is widened in migration `0136_s1_intake_retry_status`. (3) The page timeout is the existing `OCR_PAGE_TIMEOUT_MS` (its default becomes 90000 in S0-T03, `ocr.config.ts`; this card only reads it). (4) Manual accept: fix the late rejection so that accept validates only what a Draf needs and otherwise returns the failing reason at once.
- Build (absent in V3): the retry routes `POST /api/intake/files/:id/retry` and `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/retry` (the retry routes of the Data/API line; they do NOT exist, this card builds them; request body none, `202 {data: {attempt, retriesLeft}}`, 404 for an unknown id or another teacher's batch, 409 ERR-INT-11 on the 4th attempt, 409 while the file is still being processed; 60 lines 749-759, block "API-Q-26 intake retry"); the failed pages of a file are the rows `intake_pages.ocr_status = 'failed'` of that file (`intake.prisma:91`, with `error_code`), so no new page column is needed: `files/:id/retry` re-runs those pages, `batches/:id/retry` re-runs the failed pages of every file, and `extract` with `pages: [n, ...]` re-runs the named pages (each increments `intake_files.retry_count` of the file by 1); `intake_files.retry_count`; `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT_MS` (absent today, default 900000, code `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT`, added to `env.schema.ts` by the cross-sector PR to S0). The batch timer starts when the batch enters `processing` and is stopped by a sweep job in the jobs worker (name it `intake-batch-timeout` in `apps/api/src/modules/jobs`), not by the request. Retry auth: Admin Pusat for any batch, a teacher only for the teacher's own batch (`intake-teacher.gate.ts`). User messages (40 X10.11): ERR-INT-05 "Pengecaman teks mengambil masa terlalu lama." (button `Cuba lagi`); ERR-INT-11 "Fail ini gagal diproses berulang kali. Sila hubungi sokongan."
- Decided (D115): the 4th retry is refused as above. The batch end rule is written once in 45 section 3.3.2 item 7 and is: when processing ends the status is `selesai_dengan_ralat` if at least 1 page failed and at least 1 item was extracted, `failed` if 0 items were extracted or `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT` was hit, else `ready_for_review` (shown "Selesai").
- Manual `Terima` (D115 default; verified): `POST /api/intake/items/:id/accept` (`review.service.ts:142` region) runs the FULL ready-to-publish list `itemIssues` (`intake/workflow/gate.ts:47`: scheme total, unit, SP, TP, alt text, figure) at line 521 before it creates the Draf, so a Terima with a missing unit or SP is refused late and the batch is left at 0 questions. Fix: add `draftIssues(q, stimulus, facts)` in `gate.ts` returning only what a Draf needs, which is what `submitGate` needs (`workflow/lifecycle/gates.ts`): the stem or a part prompt is not empty (`G.stemEmpty`), the item is not still `needsManual` (`G.needsManual`), subject and level are known (`G.subjectLevelMissing`), and a named stimulus exists (`G.stimulusMissing`). `accept` calls `draftIssues` instead of `itemIssues`; on any issue it answers 422 at once with `fields`/`details.gate[]` of `{itemId, where, message}` ("Soalan {no}: {mesej}") and creates nothing; unit, SP, TP, scheme and the rest are then asked by the gates at `Hantar untuk semakan`/`Terbitkan` (S1-T02). `itemIssues` stays for the review summary counters.
- PRD: NFR-7, FR-ADM-Q07.2, AC-ADM-Q07.1, FR-ADM-Q09.3 and the manual `Terima` behaviour of FR-ADM-QE4.3 (Terima validates only what a Draf needs; the blur validation of a free-text SP is S1-T02's; the rest of R1-ADM-Q-09 is S1-T03's, the rest of R1-ADM-QE-04 is S1-T02's) (FR-ADM-Q07.5, the worker and OCR runtime, is S0-T03's). Gap: "Sedang diproses" forever; manual "Terima" rejected late then batch shows 0 questions ("Hantar untuk semakan" disabled).
- Data/API: DM-71; API-Q-26
- Also uses: API-Q-14, API-Q-15 (owned by another card; read or verified here, not an ownership claim)
- New requirements (45, 46, 48; D113): FR-QA-38; AC-QA-38.
- Approach: job timeout (90 s per page, code `OCR_TIMEOUT`; 15 min per batch, code `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT`; both ERR-INT-05) → batch state `gagal` with BM reason and a retry (3 per file, ERR-INT-11, `intake_files.retry_count`), and the partial state `Selesai dengan ralat` (migration `0136_s1_intake_retry_status`, DM-71); manual Terima creates the drafts or tells why. Retry (one path): failed files or pages are retried only through the two retry routes BUILT HERE (`POST /api/intake/files/:id/retry`, `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/retry`), each counting toward the 3 retries per file (ERR-INT-11); `extract` (API-Q-15) starts the first extraction of a batch and, with `pages[]`, retries chosen pages. The status `selesai_dengan_ralat` ("Selesai dengan ralat") is in API-Q-14 and OPT-BATCH-STATUS. The 90 s and 15 min limits are two settings read by `intake-processing.service.ts`: `OCR_PAGE_TIMEOUT_MS` (exists in `env.schema.ts` with no default; the runtime default 90000 is set by S0-T03 in `ocr.config.ts`, error code `OCR_TIMEOUT`) and `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT_MS` (new, default 900000, error code `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT`); the names `OCR_PAGE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` and `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT_MINUTES` are withdrawn. `env.schema.ts` is S0's file: this card adds `OCR_BATCH_TIMEOUT_MS` by a small cross-sector PR to S0 (`Cross-sector: S0 (env.schema.ts)`, `needs:S0`, merged first). S0-T03 owns `ocr/**` (the existing `TesseractEngine` in `ocr-engine.ts`) and its new `jobs/jobs.health.ts`; this card builds neither. A retry count of 3 means three retries AFTER the first attempt (`retry_count` 0 to 3; the 4th retry is refused with 409 ERR-INT-11). The stored batch statuses are those of OPT-BATCH-STATUS in 40 (`submitted`, `processing`, `ready_for_review`, `selesai_dengan_ralat`, `failed`, `cancelled`); 40 X10.5.3 maps its job words onto them. The 15-minute clock starts when the batch enters `Sedang diproses` (45 section 3.3.2 item 6). Retry (the one rule of 45 section 3.3.2 item 6): one failed page is retried through `extract` with `pages[]` (API-Q-15), one failed file through `POST /api/intake/files/:id/retry` and all failed pages of a batch through `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/retry` (the retry API of 60); every one of the three counts toward the 3 retries per file (`intake_files.retry_count`, the same counter whichever route) and the 4th is refused with 409 ERR-INT-11. The stored batch status is `failed` (shown "Gagal"; this plan's word `gagal` means that status; a finished batch is stored `ready_for_review`, shown "Selesai"); `Cuba lagi` is offered only for `failed` and `selesai_dengan_ralat`, never for a cancelled batch (shown "Dibatalkan"). The manual `Terima` that rejects late is fixed only here. Files: `BE/intake/files/intake-processing.service.ts`, `BE/intake/workflow/**`, `CT/intake`, `apps/api/src/common/config/env.schema.ts` (cross-sector S0, the one new key). Tests: timeout unit test; the limits are the same everywhere (90 s per page, 15 min per batch, 3 retries per file; AC-ADM-Q07.1: a test PDF of up to 5 pages reaches "Selesai" within 5 minutes and no batch is still "Sedang diproses" after 15 minutes). Verify: V-STD; kill the worker and watch the error appear. DoD: DoD-T.

This card edits API-Q-03 only in `types.ts`, `grading.ts` and the scheme validation.
- Approach: (1) the 11 question types of OPT-JENIS-SOALAN (40, the master list; FR-ADM-QE3.2 and DM-69 agree) in one `QUESTION_TYPES` constant, in this order: `mcq`, `mcq_multi`, `true_false`, `match`, `short_answer`, `fill_blank`, `structured`, `working`, `essay`, `drawing`, `table_fill`. `short_text` and `numeric` are LEAF MODES (OPT-MOD-JAWAPAN, 9 modes), never question types; the table `questionType` to default leaf mode(s) sits next to OPT-JENIS-SOALAN in 40 (`mcq` to `mcq`; `mcq_multi` to `mcq` multi; `true_false` to `mcq` with 2 options Betul/Salah; `match` to `match_letter`; `short_answer` to `short_text` or `numeric`; `fill_blank` to `short_text`, one leaf per gap; `structured` to any leaf modes per part; `working` to `working`; `essay` to `essay` or `essay_choice`; `drawing` to `drawing`; `table_fill` to `table_fill`). The 14 V2 modes map onto the 11 types by the migration map of 30 QE3.8; `mcq_multi` and `true_false` are question types and never leaf kinds: their leaves use leaf mode `mcq` with `multi: true` (`mcq_multi`) or two fixed options Betul/Salah (`true_false`) ; the answer-key kind `options` is `{correctOptionIds: Id[]}` only (no `choices` and no `choice` field for it); the other key kinds keep the fields of 45 section 7.0; MCQ has 2 to 8 options (default 4); `mcq_multi` has 2 to 8 options with 1..(n-1) correct, and the key and the reveal use the field `correctOptionIds: Id[]` everywhere (DM-69, API-BIJAK-06 and the reveal; migration `0134_s1_question_types`). (2) Scheme v2 in `answer_keys.content` (`scheme_v: 2`) and `question_versions.scheme_rules` (DM-54), and `question_parts.mode_config` (DM-55, migration `0133_s1_scheme_v2_mode_config`): point kinds K/N/P/B/M/F/C, "@" alternatives, "Maks N", groups with caps and bonus, Ciri/Sebab pairs, paths, count-based marks, deductions and zero rules, bands and word ranges, `essay_choice` with 2 to 6 prompts, `Templat jawapan (pra-isi)`, "Bergantung pada jawapan bahagian" (carried-value points), `Had perkataan`, two accepted options with a note. The field names of the scheme v2 object are those of 45 section 7 (normative; they supersede `docs/requirements/v2-exam-schema.md` D4 wherever the two differ); DM-54, DM-55 and the scheme validate route cite 45 section 7 and use its names, and "best reachable marks with caps" is the algorithm worked in 45 section 7. The matcher kinds of 46 M-WORK (`step`, `all_of`, `final`, `unit`, `keywords`, `manual`) are read through the point fields of 45 section 3.1.7 by the mapping table that 46 M-WORK holds; `grading.ts` implements that table and nothing else. Marks are 1..100; 0 only for an example leaf (45 L-04 wins over 46 section 3.1 "0 or more"). (3) The route `POST /api/questions/:id/parts/:partId/scheme/validate` (the Data/API line) returns `{ok, issues[{path, code, message, step}]}` so the editor shows every problem live. (4) Grading and marking for each kind, the P1 modes `match_letter` and `working`, "Pilih N" (a group is one choice unit); essays and unparseable numbers become `belum_dimarkah`.
- Files: `BE/questions/{grading.ts,types.ts,publish-gate.ts}`, `BE/questions/served-rule.ts` (S1-T07's file, same sector S1: the `mcq_multi` exclusion of AC-PA-35 is added to it here by rule (b); S1-T07 is a dependency for that reason), `SCH/questions.prisma`, `CT/questions`; `packages/exam-core/**` only by a small cross-sector PR to S3 (`Cross-sector: S3 (exam-core Pilih N and kinds)`, `needs:S3`). **"Pilih N" has one owner: this card** owns the rule (a group is one choice unit) in `grading.ts` and `exam-core`; S3-T02 only places the section-rule control of the Set Builder and S1-T10 does not touch it.
- Tests: table-driven grading per kind and per scheme kind, with the worked examples of 45 section 7 as fixtures; scheme validation names every issue with its step; mcq_multi bounds; an old V2 mode maps onto the new kind.
- Verify: V-STD with the S8-T03 widgets and the S2-T10 forms. DoD: DoD-T + the IDs of table 4.0 E for this card.
