**S1-T10 Accepted batch becomes a draft paper (Semak & Terbit)** · P0 · M · Type: Build · deps: S1-T03, S1-T04
- Starts from (existing): `apps/api/src/modules/exam/builder/{builder.service.ts,builder.controller.ts,paper-check.ts,section-pool.service.ts}`, `apps/api/src/modules/intake/workflow/{review.service.ts,to-question.ts,grouping.ts}`, `apps/api/prisma/schema/exam.prisma`, `packages/contract/src/bijak/builder/schema.ts`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): `builder.service.ts` writers `create` (line 451, paper), `addSection` (1453), `addPlacement` (939), `reorderSection` (1001), `setGroupLabel` (1411); `paper-check.ts`; `papers.paper_code` already exists as a nullable String (`exam.prisma:170`); no `source_batch_id`; the intake controllers have no batch -> paper route.
- Change: DM-45 adds only `papers.source_batch_id` and the partial unique index on (`source_batch_id`, `paper_code`) (migration `0163`); `paper_code` is NOT added. Prerequisites with numbers: `intake_items.section_label` is `0123_s1_section_label` and `intake_batches.paper_code` is `0125_s1_intake_level` (both S1-T03). The guard `if paper.surface='subjektif' then 422` depends on a column that does not exist before `0161`: test it with one `information_schema.columns` check at start-up (skip while the column is absent). The `imported=true` filter and the summary fields are edited in `exam/builder/builder.controller.ts` and `BijakBuilderPaperSummary` in `packages/contract/src/bijak/builder/schema.ts` (S3's files, small cross-sector PR).
- Build (absent in V3): `batch-to-paper.service.ts` calling the builder functions listed above (verify the names before use; no second paper writer), `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/paper`, `targetPaperId` on the batch payload.
- New files: `apps/api/src/modules/intake/workflow/batch-to-paper.service.ts`, `apps/api/prisma/migrations/0163_s3_paper_source_batch/migration.sql` (S3 range, REQUESTED from the team that holds S3 with the label `needs:schema` (A-15, run 5): that team writes the file and the Prisma model line in `SCH/exam.prisma` in a schema PR whose body names card S1-T10 and DM-45, and merges it first; migration `0163` is written by the S3 holder at S1-T10's request (decision 7), like `0127`; if no team holds S3, the S1-T10 team writes `0163` itself in a PR labelled `needs:S3` that the product owner reviews (decision 12(c), section 0B, FAQ Q10); the BE PR waits for the schema PR).
- PRD: R1-ADM-Q-07 (FR-ADM-Q07.6, AC-ADM-Q07.3 and AC-ADM-Q07.4 only, table 4.0 E), FR-X1-4e (no other ID of R1-ADM-Q-15 or R1-ADM-QE-05: table 4.0 E gives them to S3-T02 and S1-T03). Gap: no FR, endpoint, table link or card turned an accepted intake batch into a paper: the intake controller has extract / review / items / stimuli / accept / reject / duplicate only, `Kertas Diimport` lists nothing and `Semak & Terbit` has nothing to open (audit F-01, D-A2).
- Data/API: DM-45; API-Q-25
- Also uses: API-Q-14, API-Q-15, API-BIJAK-10, DM-07, DM-11, DM-23 (owned by another card; read or verified here, not an ownership claim)
- New requirements (45, 46, 48; D113): FR-QA-41; AC-QA-41.
- New requirements from the audit (this card owns them): FR-ADM-Q07.6 and its ACs (AC-ADM-Q07.3, AC-ADM-Q07.4). The `Semak & Terbit` preview and blueprint validation (FR-ADM-Q07.7, FR-ADM-Q07.8, AC-ADM-Q07.5, AC-ADM-Q07.6) are Semak Kertas (R1-ADM-Q-15) and belong to S3-T02; the tab UI of FR-ADM-Q07.9 is S2-T02, and this card adds the list filter it reads (below).
- Approach: `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/paper` in the intake module creates ONE draft `papers` row per batch (an upload carries one paper code, U-09; to import two paper codes the admin uploads twice) (header from the batch; `surface = 'simulasi'` is written only when the migration `0161` column exists, `exam_board` stays null, see "Columns of 0161" below), one `paper_sections` row per distinct item Bahagian in order of first appearance (`label`; the rule `semua` or `pilih N` is copied from the Disahkan contract section whose label matches the printed Bahagian, see "Bahagian to section" below) and `paper_placements` in printed order with `number_label`, each stimulus group placed whole and contiguous in one section, by calling the existing builder services in `exam/builder/` (no second paper writer). Idempotent per batch and paper code: the table has a unique index on (`source_batch_id`, `paper_code`) (DM-45, L-5), so a second call never creates a second paper. Paper code (45 section 3.5): the upload's `Kod kertas`; when the upload gave none, `paper_code` = `K{kertas}` (for example `K1`), so it is never empty; the column is NULL on a paper not created from a batch and the unique index covers the rows where `source_batch_id` is not null. Bahagian to section: one section per distinct item Bahagian (`intake_items.section_label`, a column that S1-T03's migration `0123_s1_section_label` adds and the intake item row of 50 (DM-07) registers, P-12, no new DM ID; detected or edited in Semak Ekstrak, with the one Bahagian typed at upload U-04 as the default), in order of first appearance; none at all gives one section "Bahagian A". Contract match (S-3): a printed Bahagian is matched to a Disahkan contract section by label, trimmed and case-insensitive ("Bahagian A" equals "bahagian a"); the matched section's `semua` or `pilih N` is copied to the new section; no match gives `semua`. Re-call rule: a second call on a batch that already has a paper only appends placements for items accepted since the first call (printed order, at the end of their section; missing sections are created) and never removes, reorders or edits anything the admin changed in Set Builder; it answers 409 "Kertas ini sudah diterbitkan. Tarik balik ke draf dahulu." when the paper is `Terbit`. Item states (D115 default; the states are those of `IntakeItemState`, `review.service.ts` counts): `accepted` (has a `questionId`, created by `accept`) is placed; `rejected` and `duplicate` are decided and never placed; "pending" means `generated` or `needs_review`, and any pending item makes the call answer 409 "Masih ada soalan yang belum diterima atau ditolak." and create nothing. Placements use the `questionId` that `accept` stored on the item, so this service never creates a question. Split-group rule: a group is placed whole with its accepted members; a rejected member is not placed, a group with no accepted member is not placed, and the 422 applies only when a placement would separate accepted members of one group. API-Q-25 is for `surface = simulasi` only: for a batch that is the source batch of a Kertas Subjektif paper (created by API-BIJAK-14, which already targets a `subjektif` paper) it answers 422 "Kertas subjektif tidak dijana daripada kelompok." and creates nothing. **Guard line (owner: this card, round 5):** the line `if paper.surface = 'subjektif' then 422` and its test are written by THIS card, as soon as the column exists they are live; before `0161` lands no paper has `surface = 'subjektif'`, so the guard tests the batch through `papers.source_batch_id` pointing at a paper whose `surface` is `subjektif` once the column exists and is skipped while the column does not (a feature check on the column, no flag). **Columns of 0161 (P-13, decided):** `surface` and `exam_board` arrive with S3-T06's migration `0161` (Sprint 4) while this card is Sprint 2, so S3-T06 cannot be in `deps:`; this card's service writes `surface = 'simulasi'` only when that column exists (one column-exists check at start-up), and until S3-T06 merges the papers are created without it; migration `0161` backfills `surface = 'simulasi'` only, for every paper with `source_batch_id` not null; `exam_board` stays null for imported papers (an admin sets it). The `apply` route (`POST /api/admin/papers/:id/source` with `apply: true`, API-BIJAK-14) and `paper-source.service.ts` are S3-T06's; the button `Hantar untuk semakan` of the batch is S2-T02's and it picks the route by `targetPaperId` of the batch payload: API-BIJAK-14 apply when it is set (the batch is a paper's source batch), otherwise API-Q-25. A refusal for a split group is 422 with `fields.items` "Soalan dalam kumpulan rangsangan mesti dipilih bersama." (ERR-QST-02; the 60 shape wins, the group name is in `details.group`). This card also adds the additive filter `imported=true` to `GET /api/admin/papers` (API-BIJAK-10, owner S3-T02; a small PR to S3 with `Cross-sector: S3 (papers list filter)` and `needs:S3`): papers whose `source_batch_id` is set, newest first, 20 per page; it is the list of `Kertas Diimport`. **Summary fields (A-19, owner: this card):** in the same small PR to S3 this card adds the additive fields `questionCount`, `paperCode`, `sourceBatchId`, `importIssueCount` and `status` to `BijakBuilderPaperSummary` (60 section 2.17.4) for that tab; S2-T02 depends on S1-T10 and needs no S3-T02 dependency for them. The UI is S2-T02 (tab `Kertas Diimport`, link `Semak & Terbit`); publishing stays S3-T02 (Set Builder, Disahkan contract). S1-T10 adds the field `targetPaperId` (the id of the paper whose `source_batch_id` is this batch, else null) to the batch payload of API-Q-14 and API-Q-15 (60 §2.17.4).
- Files: new `BE/intake/workflow/batch-to-paper.service.ts` (named here), `BE/intake/workflow/**` controller route, call site in `BE/exam/builder/` (S3 review), `CT/intake`; migration `0163_s3_paper_source_batch` (S3 range, `needs:schema` request to S3, DM-45; if no team holds S3 this card's team writes it in a PR labelled `needs:S3`, product owner reviews).
- Tests: batch with sections A (10 items) and B (5 items) of which items 5-8 form a group gives 1 draft paper, 2 sections, 15 placements, items 5-8 adjacent; a second call appends only and creates no second paper; a pending item gives 409; a split group gives 422; a batch that is the source batch of a Kertas Subjektif paper gives 422 (this card's test, run once `0161` exists; S3-T06's PR re-runs it).
- Verify: V-STD at 1280/768/375 from upload (S2-T02) to `Semak & Terbit`; AC-CHAIN-2. DoD: DoD-T + AC-ADM-Q07.3 and AC-ADM-Q07.4 (the import route and the draft paper; AC-ADM-Q07.5 and 07.6, the preview and publish, are S3-T02's).

`POST /api/admin/papers/:id/source` with `apply: true` (API-BIJAK-14, S3-T06's route) when `targetPaperId` is set (the batch is the source batch of a Kertas Subjektif paper), otherwise `POST /api/intake/batches/:id/paper` (API-Q-25, S1-T10's route); the API-BIJAK-14 branch is part 2 of this card, wired after S3-T06 has merged (until then the button calls API-Q-25 only); the tab `Kertas Diimport` also reads the extra summary fields S1-T10 adds to API-BIJAK-10; `submissions-unavailable.ts` is deleted by this card alone (no Studio page imports it); `Hantar untuk semakan` is enabled when no item is `pending` and at least one is accepted (45 section 3.3.6; this wins over "at least one draft" wording of FR-ADM-Q09.4, which means the same state); review screen lets the reviewer change the group range (add/remove member) before Terima; PDF pane + editor become tabs under 1024 px (FR-X6-11); busy/failed/timeout states. **Teacher items and the pre-fill (D113-continued, L-6; this card owns it):** every teacher submission is an intake item with `source = guru` (S1-T08, API-GUR-17/18); the tab `Kiriman Guru` lists them: its rows and its tiles are intake ITEMS with `source=guru` (the `source=guru` filter of API-Q-15, which PRD60 now lists; S1-T08 builds it on the batch `source`, DM-72), counted by OPT-KIRIMAN-STATUS; they are not batches (`SubmissionListPage.vue` is changed to read this route, since no `/api/question-submissions` route exists). The tiles `Pending`, `Processing`, `Selesai`, `Ditolak` and `Jumlah` and the filter use the ONE mapping of FR-ADM-Q12.1 (45 §3.4: `Pending` = item pending, `Processing` = item accepted but question not yet `Diluluskan`, `Selesai` = question `Diluluskan`, `Ditolak` = item with `rejection_reason` set; there is no stored `rejected` status, the stored item statuses are those of OPT-KIRIMAN-STATUS in 40: `pending`, `accepted`, `approved`, `draft` plus `rejection_reason`); API-Q-15 accepts `status` with those stored values and `kirimanStatus` with the display keys of OPT-KIRIMAN-STATUS, mapped on the server; group header actions are `Pisahkan` and `Lampirkan` (30 Q-09 field 09-05); the TEACHER sees Menunggu Semakan (item pending or accepted and not yet published), Diluluskan only after `Terbitkan`, and Ditolak with the reason. `Tolak` on a teacher item is `POST /api/intake/items/:id/reject` (API-Q-15 `.../reject`, not API-Q-06, which is the question status route) and needs a reason of 5 to 500 characters, stored as `rejection_reason`; on an item of an admin paper batch the reason is optional (at most 200) and `Semak` / `Terima` (API-Q-15) creates a `Draf` question and opens the Studio wizard `/admin/studio/questions/:id/sunting` with that Draf's id; the pre-fill IS the Draf that Terima created from the submitted fields (Subjek, Tingkatan, stem, options and correct answer, the teacher's 1 to 5 star difficulty as `difficultyLevel`; TP, Bab, SP and aras stay empty for the admin, FR-ADM-Q12.6). Missing DSKP fields never block Terima (for teacher items and for paper items alike: Terima refuses only an invalid typed value, such as a free-text SP, with the field name, FR-ADM-Q09.3); only `Terbitkan` is blocked, with the gate list (AC-ADM-Q12.3). The `Kiriman Guru` tab needs the backend of S1-T08 (Sprint 4); until it merges the tab shows the empty text "Tiada kertas soalan dihantar oleh guru lagi." and the ACs AC-ADM-Q12.* on teacher items are `[UAT]`, proven after S1-T08.   - Which contracts: only versions in status `Disahkan` (API-BIJAK-02 `canGenerate`); the script is READ-ONLY and never creates a paper: no call of `POST /api/admin/papers`, `POST /api/admin/papers/generate` or any publish route and no `--apply` flag. The dry run reads the existing pool preview `GET /api/admin/papers/pool-preview?subject={code}&kertas={n}` (`builder.controller.ts:35`, same candidate list and code as Jana set, `composeStandardPaper`; response `BijakPoolPreview`: `verdict` `boleh` | `tidak_boleh` | `tidak_pasti`, `firstFailure`, `sections[]`, `eligibleTotal`, `publishedTotal`, `excluded`). (`POST /api/admin/papers/preview` is a different route, a question-id review, not used.)
  - Dry-run report: one row per contract: `code`, `kertas`, `source` (Spec | Disahkan), `canGenerate`, `verdict`, `firstFailure` (text), `eligibleTotal`, `publishedTotal`, `wouldCreate` (`yes` only when `canGenerate` is true and `verdict` is `boleh`, else `no`); file `.evidence/terbit-papers-{date}.csv` (gitignored) and the same table on stdout. The real run (Jana set) can only fail with the ApiError codes `NO_BLUEPRINT` (422, no active blueprint or its source is Draf) and `SHORTAGE` (422, `details.shortages`), see `bijak/papers/errors.ts`; the report lists them as the `firstFailure` text and the code when a pool preview answers 404 or 422. There are no `BIJAK_GENERATE_*` codes.
  - Launch list (PRD30 U-OP-04 order): 3472/2, then 1449/1, 1249/1, 4531/1, Sains K1; plus 1449/2, 3472/1, 4531/2, 1249/2. 1249/1 and 4531/1 have no seeded/reference data (see S3-T01): the dry run lists them as "no contract". 4 of 6 seeded contracts are INCOMPLETE and not Disahkan until an admin fills KBAT.
  - `Sains K1` has no contract code in the launch list: the script matches it by subject name `Sains` and `kertas = 1` against the API-BIJAK-02 list and prints `no contract` when nothing matches. The T5 pupil check (verify) is a manual UAT step.
- PRD: gap §7 P0-1. Approach: script `apps/api/scripts/uat-ops/terbit-papers.ts` (dry-run default) that reads the pool preview for approved contracts and reports what Jana set would do (it builds nothing); documents U-OP-04/08/09; the script only produces the dry-run report; the real publish is confirmed by the user and run by the designated UAT developer or done by an admin in Set Builder (D110). Files: `apps/api/scripts/uat-ops/**` (S4 dir; S4 reviews), `docs/ops/`. Tests: dry-run report test (CI). Verify: on UAT, after the approved run, a T5 pupil sees papers in Bijak SPM. DoD: DoD-T + AC-MUR-13 "paper list non-empty".

Definitions (D115 default; node kinds are `grouping`, `unit`, `sk`, `sp` per the check constraint in `curriculum.prisma`, and the D109 tree Tingkatan -> Subjek -> Bab -> SK -> SP -> TP calls the `unit` node the Bab): `bab` = count of `curriculum_nodes` of that subject and level with `kind = 'unit'`; `sk` = `kind = 'sk'`; `sp` = `kind = 'sp'`; `tp` = count of `curriculum_tp_descriptors` rows whose `node_id` is a node of that subject and level (the unit and SK descriptors, TP 1-6); `tpDescriptors` = count of the subject's general-level rows (`node_id IS NULL`, the same number on each level row of the subject); `state` = `dimuatkan` when that subject and level has at least one `curriculum_nodes` row of any kind, else `belum dimuatkan` (the row is still returned, with zero counts); with `subjectId` and `levelId` also the node list (Bidang / SK / SP with TP descriptors) for the C-01 `DSKP` tab. S4-T07's tab uses the same read. "Offered subject" = every active subject in `subjects` linked to a Tingkatan 1-5 level via `subject_levels` (`curriculum-data/subject-levels.json`).

The synchronous constants `SPM_LEVEL_CODES` / `OFFERED_LEVEL_CODES` are deleted and each user is switched (find by symbol): `questions/learner-visibility.ts:48` (`OFFERED_LIST`, used in `SPM_SERVABLE_SQL`): replace the `ol.code IN (...)` clause by `ol.stage <> 'primary' AND ol.is_active`, which is the same rule in SQL; `questions/serving/rules.ts:68` (`spmReasons`): drop the `level_not_offered` test (a primary or inactive level is already reported by `commonReasons`); `practice/start.planner.ts:175,257,267` and `practice/reads/overview/overview.service.ts:352`: build the set of offered level ids once per request from `offeredLevels(db)`; `curriculum/curriculum.repository.ts:18` (`LEARNER_TOPIC_GRADES`) and `learner.service.ts` use the `gradeKey` list. The front-end constants (`m05-practice/components/dashboard/dashboard-lib.ts:36`, `m03-curriculum/lib/student-catalogue.ts:102`) are S5-T01's.
  - `MAP_GRADES` for T1-T3: grade keys `TINGKATAN_1..3`. The seed `apps/api/prisma/seed/curriculum-data/levels.json` has `grade` 7 to 11 for T1 to T5 (primary D1 to D6 are 1 to 6), so `TINGKATAN_<grade>` would be wrong; the key is `TINGKATAN_` + the digit of the level `code` (`T4` -> `TINGKATAN_4`, which is what `legacy_topics.grade_level` holds today). Replace the frozen `MAP_GRADES` / `DEFAULT_MAP_GRADE` (`curriculum/rules.ts:150-152`) by the `gradeKey` values of `offeredLevels(db)`; the default stays `TINGKATAN_4`.
  - Read switch `legacy_topics` -> `chapters -> topics -> subtopics` in `learner.service.ts:73,80,110`: response shapes of `GET /api/topics`, `/api/topics/:id`, `/api/learning-map` stay the same (pupil FE unchanged); list any changed field in `curriculum/schema.ts`.
  - The old level-wide message "Kandungan untuk Tingkatan anda akan datang" (PRD60 API-DSKP-01) disappears; the per-subject empty state copy is S5-T01's (cite its string).

- PRD: FR-X3-3, AC-X3-1, D101. The backend half of FR-MUR-16-13 (`rules.ts:43-45`, `OFFERED_LEVEL_CODES`) is delivered here; FR-MUR-16-13 and AC-MUR-16-9 are owned and verified by S5-T01 (10 section 4.11 item 6). Gap: `apps/api/src/modules/practice/rules.ts:41` `SPM_LEVEL_CODES = ['T4','T5']` and curriculum learner rules limit to T4/T5 (S5 owns `practice/rules.ts`; this card edits that one file through a small cross-sector PR to S5, `Cross-sector: S5 (practice/rules.ts level codes)`, `needs:S5`). This card alone changes 10 section 4.11 item 6 (find the lines by the symbols `SPM_LEVEL_CODES` and `OFFERED_LEVEL_CODES`; S5-T03 does not touch level codes). The servable-subject filter is the SQL fragment `SPM_SERVABLE_SQL` of `questions/learner-visibility.ts` (line 52), into which S1-T07 ANDs the flag predicate `NOT_FLAGGED_SQL` (flags `stimulus_missing`/`figure_only` open and not dismissed), hence the dependency below, and it is the same predicate that API-PRAC-04 (S5-T03) uses for its servable counts, so no second calculation exists; DM-20 `stage <> 'primary'` is the Tingkatan 1 to 5 filter. **S5-T03 Latih Tubi** · P0 · L · Type: Complete · deps: S1-T06, S1-T07, S1-T02, S8-T02, S5-T15, S5-T01, S4-T06

- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): config page, list page and quiz page; planner `start.planner.ts` (`latihTubi` at :252; `random` still a live case at :110/:172); `MISSION_COINS_PER_CORRECT = 3` at `rules.ts:50`, used at `rules.ts:61` and `start.planner.ts:317` (already combined with a request value `res.coinsPerCorrect || MISSION_COINS_PER_CORRECT`); difficulty chips 1..5 (`components/quiz/practice-config.ts:12-36`, label "Sgt Mudah"); count buttons 5/10/15/20 default 10 (OPT-BILANGAN-LT); existing config copy ("Pilih Tahap Pendidikan", "Pilih Tahap Kesukaran", etc.): keep as is.
- Change (Part A): (1) `start.planner.ts:259` rejects `difficulty < 2` with 422, so the chip "Sgt Mudah" (1) fails: accept 1..5 (FR-MUR-16-3). (2) `POST /api/practice/sessions` with `mode: "random"` answers 422 ERR-PRAC-07 "Mod tidak sah." (60 API-PRAC-01; the earlier "ERR-REQ" wording is wrong); remove the live `random` cases at `start.planner.ts:110/:172` (and `random()` itself; the `past_year` and `ramalan` start cases at :116/:120 are refused the same way: delete them too when a grep of `src/modules/m06-spm` and the planner tests finds no caller, else leave them unreachable) and take the three legacy values out of the START list only: in `packages/contract/src/practice/schema.ts` add `export const LEGACY_PRACTICE_MODES = ['random','past_year','ramalan'] as const` and change `PRACTICE_START_MODES` (:32) to `PRACTICE_MODES` without `pksk` and without `LEGACY_PRACTICE_MODES`; `PRACTICE_MODES` itself (10 values, widened by S5-T15) stays the read/check list, so old rows still validate and the S5-T15 check keeps the three values (D115 default). The refusal is the 422 of the start-request schema with code `ERR-PRAC-07`, message "Mod tidak sah." (set the message in `apps/api/src/modules/practice/errors.ts` next to `invalidModes`). Update `packages/contract/src/practice/__tests__/practice.test.ts:59`. (3) Replace `MISSION_COINS_PER_CORRECT` (`rules.ts:50,61`, `start.planner.ts:317`, the comment at `ports.ts:133`, mocks `pins.ts`, `mock/repository/{start,practice}.ts`) by the helper `coinsPerCorrect()` of S5-T15 (D115 default, per DM-48: one rate for EVERY mode, not only `mission`): `sessionCoins(mode, correct, rate)` becomes `correct * rate` for all modes and its caller `practice.repository.ts:499` reads `await coinsPerCorrect()` when the credit is written (at "Tamat"); `start.planner.ts:317` stops storing a fallback (`coinsPerCorrect: null`), the per-mission `coinsPerCorrect` column of `classes/missions` is no longer read for the credit (the mission's `rewardCoins` is paid once at the target, S6-T07), and the `latih_tubi` credit is 1 Syiling per correct answer until S7-T05 creates the `reward_settings` row (the helper returns 1). (4) Count buttons above the available number are disabled in `PracticeConfigPage.vue` (the `COUNT_OPTIONS` loop at :266, using the existing `available` computed at :81), each with title `Hanya {n} soalan tersedia.` (same rule and copy as S5-T02). (5) D91 counts from `overview.service.ts` equal the dashboard and the config page (AC-MUR-2-2).
- Change (Part B): `servableMcq()` in `be09-practice-mapping.ts:37-41` REFUSES every question with a group, `stimulusIds`, a leaf `stimulusId`, or an option image, so today NO figure question and NO group is ever served in drills (the earlier text "figure-less items served" is wrong; D105 is a build, not a filter fix). Build: (a) wire shape (D115 default): add to `PracticeQuizQuestionSchema` (`packages/contract/src/practice/schema.ts:132`, strict) two fields, `stimuli: Stimulus[]` (empty array when none; `Stimulus` = `{id, kind, label, text|null, caption|null, alt|null, source|null, imageId|null, table|null {head: string[], rows: string[][]}}`, the same fields as the Studio `WireStimulus` in `src/modules/m04-question-bank/lib/studio/stimulus.ts:33`; the image is shown through `/api/files/<imageId>`) and `group: {id, index, size} | null` (`index` 1-based position of this member inside the served group); `PracticeQuestion` (`ports.ts:29`) and `toPracticeQuestion` (`be09-practice-mapping.ts:53`) carry the same data and `servableMcq` (:37-41) stops refusing a group, `stimulusIds` or a leaf `stimulusId` (it keeps refusing an option image). (b) planner and sampler: a group is served whole or not at all (D90/D105): the draw picks group-aware units (a group counts as its members) and never splits a group inside the requested count; when the count cannot be met without splitting, the group is skipped. (c) the exact-count query: the SQL shapes that today require `ungrouped: true` + `mcqShape: PRACTICE_SHAPE` (the constant is defined at `be09-practice-mapping.ts:31`, used by `questions/serving/filters.ts:31,33` for the activities `latih_tubi_spm`, `databank`, `bab`, and by `Be09PracticeQuestionSource.filterOf` at `be09-practice-question-source.ts:141`, `chapterCounts` :93 and `chapterPage` :103) drop `ungrouped` and get the figure/group-aware shape; the serving parity spec of `questions/serving` must keep counts equal to draws. (d) page: `QuizRunner.vue` shows S8-T02's StimulusGroup (persistent panel, one member per screen, FR-PA-03). Option-image questions stay excluded until S1 changes the option-image refusal (`servableMcq` and the reason code `has_option_image` of `practiceShapeReasons` in `apps/api/src/modules/questions/serving/rules.ts:76`).
- Remove: the constant `MISSION_COINS_PER_CORRECT` and the `< 2` difficulty check; the `random` planner cases.

The free-trial limit (50 checked answers, `TRIAL_EXHAUSTED`, 402 ERR-PRAC-01) is described in R1-MUR-16 and must be verified in `practice.service.ts` by this card (read, not rebuilt).

(7) `reporter_role` is filled in `create` from the actor's session role (`actor.role`: `student` -> `murid`, `teacher` -> `guru`; D115 default, a user with two roles reports as the role of the current session), never from the body. (2) `/perutusan/:id` detail: title, scope chip, byline as above, `formatDateLong(createdAt)`, body (`body` is server-sanitised HTML; render it through `lib/artikel/sanitize-html.ts` of the same module inside a `whitespace-pre-line` block), link "← Perutusan" back to `/perutusan`; no reactions, comments, images, documents or video (decided below); an unknown or invisible id shows the V3 404 page (the API answers one uniform 404). (4) `/perutusan/saya`: header "Perutusan Saya"; rows from `fetchMyPerutusan` (`OwnPerutusanItem`): title, status chip `statusLabel` (Diterbitkan/Dijadualkan/Draf), `formatDateLong(createdAt)`, row links to `/perutusan/:id`; empty "Anda belum menghantar sebarang perutusan."; page size: all rows (the API is not paged). All four at 375/768/1280 without sideways scroll. (46 section 3.7), tiles "Markah {betul}/{jumlah}" (AC-MUR-19-2 "{betul}/10"), "Ketepatan {masteryPct}%", "Syiling +{n}", "Mata +{n}" (from `gamification`), then the review list: each question with "Betul" or "Salah", the chosen answer, the correct option and the explanation when the question has one; for Bahagian C the essay result is labelled `Keputusan indikatif` with the note `Markah esei ialah penilaian indikatif.` (D115 default copy; the marker stays the simulated rule); buttons "↻ Cuba Lagi" (fetches a new set) and "Senarai Domain" (to `/pksk/level/:level/domains`). Hubungi guru atau pentadbir sekolah untuk mengaktifkan pakej.` (when the SCHOOL holds the grant: FR-MUR-19-6 uses the same text); (ii) three tiles "Jumlah soalan", "Bahagian" (always 3), "Domain" (number of active domains); (iii) block "Soalan mengikut Bahagian" with rows "Bahagian A/B/C" and a count each; (iv) block "Domain teratas": the 12 domains with most questions, name and `{n} soalan`; (v) block "Pakej PKSK" with `Pakej ini diaktifkan oleh pentadbir sekolah anda.` and a STATIC display list of the five packages as observed on V2 (display only; prices are not used for billing; D115 default, FE constant in `m07-pksk/lib/levels.ts`): "PKSK Tingkatan 1 (SBP/MRSM)" RM0.00, "PKSK Tingkatan 4 (SBP/MRSM)" RM0.00, "PKSK Kombo (T1 + T4)" RM0.00, "PKSK Asas" RM39.00, "PKSK Premium" RM79.00. - Change: (1) migration 0181 also widens `ck_practice_sessions_mode` to the 12 values of API-PRAC-01 (adds `databank`, `tracking_a`; keeps the 3 legacy values) and `PRACTICE_MODES` in the contract changes with it (the three legacy values stay in it; the START list of S5-T03 excludes them); the registry has 19 values on `practice_answers` (16 writable + 3 legacy) but 12 on `practice_sessions`. (2) the two writers `practice.repository.ts:475` and `:566` call the port instead of inserting. (3) migration `0182_s5_session_abandoned`: the existing session status check is `ck_practice_sessions_status` with the values `in_progress`, `completed` (`0014_practice_write/migration.sql:60`); drop and re-create it with `in_progress`, `completed`, `abandoned` (BM label "ditinggalkan"); the check `ck_practice_sessions_completed_at` (`status = 'completed'` iff `completed_at` is set) needs no change because an abandoned session has no `completed_at`; the sweeper (D115 default) runs `update practice_sessions set status = 'abandoned' where status = 'in_progress' and <last activity> < now() - interval '2 hours'`, last activity = the newest of the session's `practice_checks.checked_at` and the session `started_at`. (4) part 2, migration `0188_s5_answer_store_validate` (the free number in the S5 range; written by THIS card, run by the normal deploy migration step, no manual step): `ALTER TABLE practice_answers VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` for the mode check and `ALTER COLUMN mode SET NOT NULL`; it ships as the card's last PR as soon as the "no write outside the port" test has an EMPTY allow-list (both practice writers move here; if another sector has added a direct writer by then, that sector's PR must route through the port first). The card is `status:done` only after 0188 is merged; the first PR (0181, 0182, port, helper, sweeper, backfill) already closes the P0 dependency of S5-T07, S5-T12 and S10-T01. (5) `coinsPerCorrect()` (`practice/coins.ts`): async; it reads `select coins_per_correct from reward_settings where id = 1` on EVERY call (an admin change must apply to the next credit, DM-48 "read when the credit is written"); only the table-presence check is cached in-process (`to_regclass('reward_settings') is not null`, cached for 60 seconds so S7-T05's migration `0223` is picked up without a restart); table or row absent -> 1; the value is clamped to 0..20.
- Remove: the direct inserts at `practice.repository.ts:475,566` (replaced by the port call).
- Build (absent in V3): port, `coins.ts`, sweeper, migrations 0181 and 0182, backfill script. Sweeper mechanics: an interval service like `AttemptSweeper` (`OnModuleInit`; period from an env var `PRACTICE_SWEEP_INTERVAL_MS`, default 5 minutes, off when `NODE_ENV=test`) (D115 default), NOT a `jobs` queue entry (the jobs module has no cron and no registration list; the "one-line registration PR to S0" is therefore not needed). Backfill script: a standalone `tsx` script using Prisma directly, run by hand by the UAT developer (no route, no auth), dry-run by default, `--apply` writes. For `abandoned`: pending `practice_checks` rows of that session are kept and never become answers (matches DM-66 "checked rows kept, no reward").

The sentences "each check writes one row" in 60 API-PRAC-02 and DM-44 are corrected. (2) sweeper style: interval service. (3) the port's allow-list starts with the two practice repository lines (see Tests).

in part 2 of this card, when the allow-list is empty, the check is validated and NOT NULL is added), and one internal `AnswerLogPort` (file `answer-log.port.ts`), whose single signature is defined in 60 section 2.17.5 and copied here verbatim: `record(entry: { userId, attemptId, questionId, partRef, mode, isCorrect, marksAwarded, marksMax, answeredAt, isTest })`, returning `Promise<{ id: string; created: boolean }>`; idempotency key (`attempt_id`, `user_id`, `question_id`, `part_ref`) (the DM-44 unique index; a null `part_ref` reads as `''`); `mode` is one of the 16 writable values of the master table "Pupil activity → stored `mode` → API → card" in 40 (after X11.3) (OPT-MODE-SESI); a second `record` with the same key updates the row and returns `created: false`, never adds one (INV-33); a write with a `mode` outside the 16 writable values (`past_year`, `ramalan`, `random` are legacy) is refused by the port before the database check; the port reads the served question version itself and copies subject, level, Bab and difficulty at write time; the two existing writers of `practice_answers` (`practice.repository.ts:475` and `:566`, inside the submit transaction) now call it (check-answer itself keeps writing `practice_checks`); for a drill session `attemptId` is the session id, so the same unique index covers drill rows, and `marksMax` is 1 for an MCQ leaf and the leaf marks otherwise (60 section 2.17.5; null only on legacy rows); expose the port to Bijak, Subjektif, Set Soalan and the games (their cards call it, 50 section 4.7a registry). The past-Bijak backfill script `apps/api/scripts/uat-ops/backfill-bijak-answers.ts` (this card owns it, a file in the S4-owned `uat-ops/` folder named on the `New files:` line) is dry-run by default. No new endpoint; no report is rewritten here (S10 and S6 read the same table).

Part 1 = the shared reader (first PR); part 2 = the Headcount block wiring, merged after S10-T02 ships API-RPT-08 `me` (ordering note, not a card dependency).

- Change: (1) `routes/prestasi.ts:29` redirects every role from `/prestasi` to `/prestasi/sekolah`: make `/prestasi` a component route (`Prestasi360Page`, userId = `useSessionStore().user.id` when the param is absent) with `beforeEnter` sending every non-student role to `/prestasi/sekolah` (teacher keeps `/prestasi/kelas` from the sidebar); a pupil's URL stays `/prestasi` (newest PRD wins: FR-MUR-4-13, AC-MUR-4-9; the "redirect to /prestasi/{ownId}" wording of the gap text and API-RPT-01 is removed). `/prestasi/:userId` with another id: API 404 and the V3 404 page (AC-MUR-4-2). (2) `routes/prestasi.ts:22` and `:26`: remove `student` from `PRESTASI_SCHOOL_ROLES` and `PRESTASI_CLASS_ROLES`; `__tests__/routes/prestasi-routes.test.ts` asserts the opposite and must be updated. (3) `performance.controllers.ts:64-106` has no `@Roles`: add `@Roles('admin','teacher','school_admin')` on `school`, `levels`, `levels/:code`, `classes`, `classes/:id` (API-RPT-02); `students/:userId` stays open to every signed-in role; `parent` is out of the prototype, keep it out. (4) `Prestasi360Page.vue:59` `const bijak = isHttpMode()` switches http mode to a reduced Bijak-only layout: remove that branch so http mode renders the normal V3 blocks (stat cards, weak topics, suggestions, map) with real data. (5) `student-performance.service.ts:32` reads only Bijak sittings and returns `weakTopics: []`: read the shared reader below, compute `weakTopics` (all time, >= 2 attempts, accuracy < 60; FR-MUR-4-8) and add `trend`. Topic resolution (D115 default): the topic of an answer is the answer row's own `practice_answers.topic_id` (the DSKP SP = contract `spId`), else its `chapter_id` (then `code` is null); rows with neither are skipped; there is no join to `questions` (the dimension columns already sit on the row, `practice.prisma` model `PracticeAnswer`). The reader rows carry `chapterId`/`topicId`, and `label`, `code` (`chapters.dskp_code`), `subjectCode` and `levelId` come from `answerFacts.spInfo` (Build (a)). `focusCount` = min(servable, 25). (6) Page title "Prestasi Saya" and sub "Analisis pencapaian latihan anda" through route meta `header`, `usePageMeta` and `ownsH1`; hide the "Kembali" link to `/prestasi/kelas` (`Prestasi360Page.vue:124`) for a pupil.
- Build (absent in V3): (a) the shared cross-mode reader (Decided D-A, D115: this card owns it, over the single store `practice_answers` with `mode`, S5-T15): port `apps/api/src/modules/reports/performance/ports/answer-facts.port.ts`, adapter `apps/api/src/modules/practice/reads/answer-facts.adapter.ts` (next to `performance-practice-read.adapter.ts`, bound in `PracticeReadsModule.onModuleInit` through a new `PerformancePorts.provideAnswerFacts`, same pattern as `providePractice`). Signature: `interface AnswerFactsQuery { userIds?: readonly string[]; schoolIds?: readonly string[]; classroomId?: string; subjectId?: string; levelCode?: 't1'|'t2'|'t3'|'t4'|'t5'; modes?: readonly string[] /* default all */; from?: Date; to?: Date }`, `interface AnswerFactRow { userId: string; subjectId: string; questionId: string; chapterId: string | null; topicId: string | null; mode: string; attempts: number; correct: number; lastAnsweredAt: Date }` (grain: user, subject, question, chapter, topic, mode), `interface WeeklyFactRow { userId: string; weekStart: string /* Monday, YYYY-MM-DD, Asia/Kuala_Lumpur */; attempts: number; correct: number; sessions: number }`, `interface SpInfo { id: string; kind: 'topic' | 'chapter'; label: string; code: string | null; subjectCode: string; levelId: string | null }`, `interface AnswerFactsPort { answerFacts(query: AnswerFactsQuery): Promise<AnswerFactRow[]>; weeklyFacts(query: AnswerFactsQuery): Promise<WeeklyFactRow[]>; spInfo(ids: { topicIds: readonly string[]; chapterIds: readonly string[] }): Promise<SpInfo[]> }` (`spInfo` reads M03 `topics.name` and `chapters.name`/`dskp_code`, one statement each; `sessions` of a week = distinct `session_id`, a Bijak sitting is one session, answers with a null `session_id` count one session per mode per MYT day (D115 default)). It reads only `practice_answers`, never returns `is_test` rows, is set-based (no per-pupil loop) and is the only place the pooled accuracy sum (correct / answered, XS-5) is made. `COUNTED_MODES = ['exam','latihan']` (`domain/constants.ts:9-15`) and the `ports.bijak.sittings/totals` reads stop feeding figures (the Bijak port stays for the sitting list and results). S10-T02 (Auto AR), S10-T03, S10-T04, S10-T07, S10-T08 and S6-T03 consume this reader and write no query of their own. (b) `trend` in the contract and service: last 12 ISO weeks (Monday start) `{week: 'YYYY-MM-DD', label: 'Mgg n', accuracy: number|null, sessions: number}[12]`, label = relative index from the oldest shown week ("Mgg 1" oldest ... "Mgg 12" current, AC-MUR-4-4), shown in a "Trend Prestasi" card using the existing V3 chart/card style with the empty copy "Belum ada data prestasi lagi. Mula latihan →"; add the type to 60 section 2.17. Source (D115 default): one `weeklyFacts` call for the pupil with `from` = Monday 00:00 (MYT) of the week 11 weeks before the current week and `to` = now; weeks without rows give `accuracy: null, sessions: 0`; `accuracy` = pooled correct / attempts of that week, one decimal. (c) Pupil ids are opaque strings in http mode (`lib/route-id.ts`) while the param is `\d+`: UAT ids are numeric strings; if a non-numeric id appears, relax the constraint.
- Decided (D115) D-B ("Sesi" list, FR-MUR-4-14): option (b). The V2 screen has no "Sesi" list region (regions 1-9 of R1-MUR-4), so no new card is built; FR-MUR-4-14 is removed from the PRD; a Bijak sitting is counted in "Sesi Kuiz" and in the Penguasaan tile "{n} sesi · {m} soalan" (FR-MUR-4-9); AC-MUR-4-8 no longer says "listed in Sesi".
- UI: keep V3 layout/components (product-owner rule 2026-11-04); V1/V2 are references for functions, data, rules, states and messages only.
- Where an older line below conflicts with the bullets above, the bullets above win (D115).
- PRD: R1-MUR-4 (all ACs, including FR-MUR-4-13, FR-MUR-4-14 and AC-MUR-4-9), FR-X4-4. Gap: pupil `Prestasi` → "Dashboard Sekolah" (school-wide data leak): `src/modules/m08-student-performance/routes/prestasi.ts:18` (`student` in `PRESTASI_SCHOOL_ROLES`), redirect `/prestasi → /prestasi/sekolah`; `/prestasi/:userId` exists unused. Approach: pupil route renders own Prestasi 360; BE rejects school-wide for student (`BE/reports/performance/domain/access-policy.ts`, `privacy.ts`). Files: those + `pages/**`. Tests: pupil gets 403 on school endpoint; own data ok. Verify: V-STD vs V2 "Prestasi Saya". DoD: DoD-T + AC-MUR-4-*.

The session mode names shown in the list (FR-MUR-4-14) are the BM names of OPT-MODE-SESI in 40.

AC timing (AC-MUR-4-8, which needs Bijak and Battle rows): in CI it is proven on fixture rows of mode `simulasi` and `battle` inserted through the S5-T15 port test double `[CI]`; on UAT it is `[UAT]`, run after the writer cards S3-T04 (`simulasi`) and S7-T01 (`battle`) have merged and been deployed; the card closes its CI part in Sprint 1 and the UAT row is written then.
- Metrics (D-A10, D-T10-1): Ketepatan is pooled (correct ÷ answered × 100) over `practice_answers` of all modes, XS-4/5/7 (60 section 4.6); API-RPT-01 and API-RPT-02 return it as a JSON number rounded to ONE decimal (decision 11: a whole value such as 50 or 100 is returned without a fraction, never with a second decimal digit) and the screen shows the whole number rounded half up (40 XS-5); Bijak sittings appear as one unit in the session list and in the counts via the single answer store.

Fields, columns, buttons and every other message of the four pages are exactly the "Forms (new, built from the rules above)" block of 20 R1-GUR-3 (G:731-736), including the Tetapan modal text "OTR{k} ke atas mempunyai markah. ### 4.S8 Question rendering and responsive

**S8-T01 Move shared display components behind one barrel** · P0 · S · Type: Fix · deps: none
- Starts from (existing): `src/components/question-display/index.ts` (barrel: 8 components + `qdCopy`, `countWords`, types), the 11 files in `src/modules/m28-bijak-spm/components/` (PartInput 293 lines, PartNode 69, QuestionBlock 190, RichText 16, MathField 357, MathKeyboard 140, StimulusStack 24, StimulusView 101, StimulusChip 31, NavigatorPanel 83, AttemptShell 52), `src/modules/m28-bijak-spm/lib/{rich-text,stimulus-data,question-display,keypad-owner}.ts`, `src/modules/m28-bijak-spm/domain/{types,paper-model,math-expr}.ts`, `eslint.config.ts:13-26`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): the barrel with 8 components (AnswerWidget, EssayAnswer, McqOptions, NotServedNotice, StimulusFigure, StimulusGroup, TextAnswer, VisibilityList). Importers of the 11 files are all inside m28 (RichText 18 files, StimulusView 10, StimulusStack 5, PartInput 3, StimulusChip 3, QuestionBlock 2, PartNode 2, MathField 2, MathKeyboard 2, NavigatorPanel 1, AttemptShell 1); nothing outside m28 imports them, so the move is low risk. `src/ds/index.ts` already re-exports question-display (`ds/__tests__/question-display-export.test.ts`).
- Change: move the 11 files to `question-display/` and export them from the barrel by their component names (`PartInput`, `PartNode`, `QuestionBlock`, `RichText`, `MathField`, `MathKeyboard`, `StimulusStack`, `StimulusView`, `StimulusChip`, `NavigatorPanel`, `AttemptShell`). ESLint `no-restricted-imports` (`eslint.config.ts:13-26`) forbids cross-module imports, so the barrel is the only allowed path for other modules. Stubs: all importers are inside m28, so they are updated mechanically and the old `.vue` paths are deleted (a `.vue` file cannot be re-exported from a `.ts` stub); the `lib/*.ts` files that move keep one-line `export * from` stubs. Display components not named in the plan but used by the paper views (`ResultGroup.vue`, `KuizItem.vue`, `PartResultCard.vue`, `ReportQuestionButton.vue`) stay in m28 (S3 owns them); `SelfMarkControl.vue` stays S3-T06's.

**S8-T02 Stimulus panel: once, sticky, zoomable** · P0 · L · Type: Complete · deps: S8-T01
- Starts from (existing): `src/components/question-display/{StimulusGroup,StimulusFigure}.vue` (A), m28 `components/{StimulusView,StimulusStack,StimulusChip,AttemptShell}.vue` (B; moved by S8-T01 to `question-display/`; the old m28 `.vue` paths no longer exist), `m28-bijak-spm/pages/AttemptPage.vue:25-38,485-489,681-694` (consumer; page edits go through S3 by a cross-sector PR), `m28-bijak-spm/pages/{PaperQuizPage,ResultPage}.vue`, `m28/components/ResultGroup.vue`, `src/modules/m04-question-bank/components/studio/StudioPreview.vue`.
- Exists in V3 (reuse; do not rebuild): TWO stimulus implementations. (A) barrel `StimulusGroup.vue` (used only by `PaperQuizPage.vue` and `StudioPreview.vue`): pinned sticky left pane from 1024 px (`:27-40`), below 1024 px a sticky chip "Lihat {label}" (`chipLabel` in `copy.ts`) opening a `UDrawer` bottom sheet (`open` model default false, :18). (B) the Bijak sitting: `AttemptPage.vue` uses `AttemptShell` (pinned aside from lg, sticky chip below lg), `StimulusStack` -> `StimulusView` -> barrel `StimulusFigure`, the drawer `stimOpen` (`AttemptPage.vue:681-694`), `StimulusChip` with a 48 px thumbnail; result groups use StimulusStack/StimulusChip. A failed image shows "Rajah tidak dapat dimuatkan." with "Cuba lagi"/"Lapor" and blocks Seterusnya (AC-07.4; `stimulusBlocked`).
- Change: Unify: `StimulusGroup` gets the prop `stimuli: StimulusData[]` (1 to 3 items in order; the old single `stimulus` prop stays for `PaperQuizPage` and `StudioPreview` and is treated as `[stimulus]`), the label is the joined labels ("Rajah 1 dan Petikan A", the `groupLabel` rule of `lib/stimulus-data.ts`), and it emits `error` and `loaded` (the events StimulusStack has today, used for the blocked-group rule). The Bijak group shape is NOT passed in: only the display type `StimulusData` crosses the barrel; the adapters `toStimulusData`/`stimuliOf` of the Bijak `StimulusDef` stay in `lib/stimulus-data.ts` (moved by S8-T01) and the S3 page calls them. `AttemptPage.vue` (sitting), `ResultGroup.vue` and the drill pages then use `StimulusGroup` and the StimulusStack/StimulusChip usage is removed, so there is ONE implementation; the Bijak page adopts it in an S3 cross-sector PR (`Cross-sector: S3`). Rules to implement in it (one definition for drill and sitting, D115 default, V3 chip + drawer kept instead of the inline 42% panel of 46 section 2.4): below 1024 px the panel header IS the chip "Lihat {label}" and the content is the existing `UDrawer`; "open" = the drawer is shown; it is open the first time a group appears in the session (the first member screen in a drill, the screen where the group first appears in a sitting) and afterwards keeps whatever state the pupil left, per group id, also on rotation (state in a small composable `useStimulusOpen(groupId)` in `question-display/lib/`, in memory for the session; FR-X6-6); full width at 768; pinned left only from 1024 px; `sourceCitation` line "Sumber: {text}" under the stimulus (FR-PA-37; today `StimulusData.source` is used only for passages); leaf-level `stimulusId` shown inside the leaf card with a collapsible "Lihat {label}" at 375; prompt links "Bahan 2" open that stimulus and "semua bahan" opens all (FR-PA-37); the matching bank shown once in the panel (FR-PA-10; with S8-T03); long maths scrolls inside (FR-X6-1).
- Build (absent in V3): zoom: a tap on a figure opens a full-screen `UModal` in `StimulusFigure.vue` (no zoom code exists) with pinch zoom, pan, double-tap (toggle fit / 2x), a close button "Tutup" (`qdCopy.close`) of at least 44 px, Back (browser or device) closes the viewer (push a history state while open), the alt text announced (46 section 2.4); keep-state storage = component state keyed by group id for the session; whole-group exclusion with the reason line "Tidak dihidangkan kerana {reason}." (`notServedLine` in `copy.ts`) and the admin "Dilihat murid di" row for a kind that cannot render (no code today); the kinds `map`, `infographic`, `graph` render as a picture or a table via `StimulusFigure` and need `src` or `table`, otherwise the group is excluded (reads DM-03).
- Remove: `StimulusStack.vue` and `StimulusChip.vue` (now in `question-display/`) once `AttemptPage.vue` and `ResultGroup.vue` use `StimulusGroup`, together with their barrel exports (S8-T01 left no stubs for `.vue` files).

So: extend `McqOptions`/`AnswerWidget` for `mcq_multi` (checkbox list of 2-8 options; served only where the answer is `choices[]`) and `true_false` (fixed options A "Betul", B "Salah", never shuffled) (FR-PA-02; the answer is a map keyed like the accepted values `key.cells` (`r{row}c{col}`, 1-based, e.g. `r2c2`) to the typed text, and the exact wire field is the one S1-T09b defines (this card reads it from the contract, it does not invent one); the pad is exported as a PNG merged with the template (at most 5 MB) and goes through the SAME photo path as a camera photo (`lib/photo-store.ts` `putPhoto`, `answer.photos[]`, upload route `POST /bijak/attempts/:id/photos`, which already accepts `drawing`; limits `MAX_PHOTO_BYTES`, `MAX_PHOTOS_PER_PART = 3`), so the leaf answer value is the photo list, no new field; tools "Pen", "Garis", "Pemadam", "Buat Asal", "Kosongkan" (46; supersedes the shorter tool list in Decided 3 below); word-limit rules (FR-PA-35: input past `max` is ignored, paste is cut at `max` words, hint `Had {max} patah perkataan dicapai.`; FR-PA-06 live word count and range hint never block Hantar; cloze optional cap "Maksimum {n} patah perkataan."); template block read-only above the answer (FR-PA-38); matching bank panel (FR-PA-10); bilingual content `{ms,en}` stacked BM above English with chrome staying BM (FR-PA-04); per-criterion steppers (FR-QA-20) are S3-T06's UI (this card only needs the `criteria` contract field); touch targets 44 px (FR-X6-3); the on-screen keyboard must not hide the field or Hantar (FR-X6-5/8).
- Remove: nothing.
- Build (absent in V3): TableFillGrid, ClozeGaps (inline gaps, each gap a leaf, inputs or selects in place), DrawingPad, LanguageToggle, the mcq_multi/true_false widgets, the template block, the matching bank, the hard word limit, the markup renderer and sanitiser.
- Decided (D115): (1) rich-text storage format: a small HTML subset sanitised on render with a copy of the artikel sanitiser pattern (45 says "sanitiser allow-list"): tags `b i u s ul ol li code table thead tbody tr th td br p`; attributes: `colspan` and `rowspan` (positive integers up to 12) on `th`/`td` only, and `class` on any allowed tag restricted to the values `poem`, `two-col`, `box`; everything else (scripts, styles, links, event attributes, unknown tags) is removed, keeping the text; the markers survive as text and are replaced after sanitising: gap markers `{{gap:N}}` (also inside a table cell) become the inline gap of `ClozeGaps`, inline maths `$latex$` and block maths `$$latex$$` (on its own line) become KaTeX output, and the inline image marker `{{img:<token>}}` becomes `<img>` with `imageSrc(token)` (m04 `lib/image-url.ts` helper; the token comes from `uploadStudioImage`, purpose `question_image`; copy the one-line URL builder instead of importing across modules if the lint forbids it, alt text empty). The Studio editor keeps plain text until S2-T10 adds the toolbar (S2-T10 writes exactly the two markers `$$...$$` and `{{img:<token>}}`, so this card renders both). `renderRich`/`renderText` in `question-display/lib/rich-text.ts` today split only on a single `$`: extend it to split first on `$$...$$` and call the existing `renderMath(latex, true)` for those (display mode), then on single `$` as before; an odd or unmatched `$$` is shown literally 46 FR-PA-36 and 45 FR-QA-18 are corrected to name this subset. (2) Language toggle: segmented control with labels "BM", "English", "BM + English" in the question header, remembered in `localStorage` key `pp.qd.lang` (D115 default; the labels are new copy, D94: flagged below). (3) DrawingPad scope: the tools of 46 section 2.2.2 (colour and line width fixed); the saved PNG goes through the existing photo path (see Change).
- Blocked on product decision: approval of the new copy "BM", "English", "BM + English" (D94); build proceeds with these labels (not a stop).
- PRs (12d allows splitting; ship in this order): (1) rich-text renderer + `sanitize.ts` + `$$`/`{{img}}` markers; (2) `AnswerWidget`/`McqOptions` for `mcq_multi` and `true_false`; (3) `TableFillGrid` + `ClozeGaps`; (4) `DrawingPad`; (5) `LanguageToggle`, template block, matching bank, word-limit rules; each with its widget tests.
- UI: keep V3 layout/components; new widgets follow the existing PartInput style.
- New files: `src/components/question-display/DrawingPad.vue` (Papan Lukisan), `src/components/question-display/TableFillGrid.vue`, `src/components/question-display/ClozeGaps.vue` (`{{gap:N}}` in place, read-only example gap), `src/components/question-display/LanguageToggle.vue` (FR-PA-04), `src/components/question-display/lib/sanitize.ts` (rich-text sanitiser), exported from the barrel `index.ts`. (`EssayChoice.vue` and `MatchLetterBank.vue` are dropped from the list: the `essay_choice` and `match_letter` branches already exist in `PartInput.vue` :158 and :128; extraction is optional refactoring.)

The widgets for `mcq_multi` (checkbox list, 2 to 8 options) and `true_false` (two fixed options Betul/Salah) are built inside the existing `AnswerWidget.vue` and `McqOptions.vue` by this card (FR-PA-02 is shared with S1-T09a, whose part is the backend: the key and `served-rule.ts`); no new file is needed for them. - Starts from (existing): `NavigatorPanel.vue` (83 lines) and `AttemptShell.vue` (52), which S8-T01 moves from m28 `components/` to `src/components/question-display/` (the paths in this card after that move are the new ones), m28 `pages/AttemptPage.vue:60-110,436-725`, `src/ds/DsQuizHeader.vue`, `src/ds/useMedia.ts`.

- Change (checklist; fix what fails): (1) at 320x568, 375x667, 667x375 (landscape) and 768 no overlap of header, banner and footer with the question (AC-X6-1; screenshots). At landscape heights of about 320-400 px the three shrink-0 bars can leave less than one line of scroll area: collapse the header title row and make the banner inline there. (2) rotation keeps the current question and scroll position (FR-X6-10): add a test and store the position in component state outside the subtree that switches with `phone`/`lg` (so the switch does not remount it), plus `sessionStorage` key `pp.qd.pos.{attemptId}` = `{questionIndex, scrollTop}` for a reload (D115 default). (5) Hantar reachable on a phone in both modes: the map sheet shows Hantar only for exams (`v-if="isExam"`, `AttemptPage.vue:712-721`) and the latihan footer shows "Selesai" only on the LAST question (`AttemptPage.vue:655-664`, the `v-else` of `!isLast`), so a phone pupil in latihan cannot finish early. Decided (D115 default): early finish IS allowed in latihan; add a "Selesai" button to the map sheet for latihan (same dialog as the footer button, `data-testid=submit-from-map`), keep "Hantar" for exams, and test both are reachable at 320x568 and 375x667. (6) with the on-screen keyboard open the footer buttons stay visible (FR-X6-5/8).

FR-PA-14, 15 and AC-PA-21, 22 (46 sections 2.7, 2.8): VERIFY-and-fix, not Build: the V3 sitting already has the server clock offset (`AttemptPage.vue:236`), the 5-minute warning (`lowTime`, `warned`, banner "Amaran: masa berbaki kurang 5 minit."), the offline save status (`autosave.ts` `SaveStatus` incl. `offline`) and a held submit that could not reach the server (`AttemptPage.vue:77`). Jawapan akan dihantar apabila sambungan pulih.`, submit on reconnect) and AC-PA-22 (Hantar dialog copy, focus on the safe button, Esc chooses it) and fixes the UI part that fails; the 24 h late-sync acceptance on the server is S3-T04's.

Two-pane to tabs is done page-locally (IntakeReviewPage and SetBuilderPage); there is no shared tab-switch primitive and DsSplit only stacks.
- Change: "table to cards" and "forms" are done: this card only audits them against FR-X4-4/FR-X6-4 (primary actions reachable) and FR-X6-5 (keyboard does not hide the active field or submit; sticky bars do not cover content) and fixes defects found: a defect is a failed item of this fixed checklist at 375 / 768 / 1280 (no horizontal page scroll, every primary action visible and >= 44 px, `DsDataTable` cards keep all columns reachable, `DsDrawer` closes by button and Esc and does not trap content, sticky bars and the keyboard do not cover the active field or submit); anything else is out of scope.

## 5. Parallelisation, critical path, sprint plan

### 5.1 What can run at the same time
- **Day 1, no dependencies:** S0 (T01–T04, T08), S1 (T01, T08), S2 (T03, T07 UI work), S3 (T01, T03), S4 (T02, T05), S5 (T05, T08), S6 (T04, T05, T07), S7 (T02), S8 (T01, T06).
- **After S0-T02 (stack):** S4-T01 CI seed; S1/S3/S5/S10 integration tests (CI).
- **After S8-T01:** S8-T02/T03/T04 in parallel with S3 admin cards (S3-T01..T03) and S2 cards.
- Sectors never write the same file (§2); shared files go through S0 (§3).

### 5.2 Critical path (Delivery A)
The path is derived from the `deps:` of the cards (the `deps:` win over any list in this plan; the sprint lists in §5A are checked against them).
- **Longest chain (about 30 ideal days):** `S1-T01 groups` → `S1-T09a/S1-T09b answer modes, scheme editor, bilingual, drawing/table_fill` → `S8-T03 answer widgets` → `S3-T04 pupil sitting` → `S3-T06 Kertas Subjektif and self-mark` → `S5-T11`.
- **Platform and Bijak chain:** `S0-T02 stack` → `S1-T01` → `S1-T03 extraction` → `S2-T02 import UI`; `S1-T06 group-safe assembly` → `S3-T02 Set Builder` → `S3-T04`; `S8-T01 → S8-T02 → S8-T03/T04` → `S3-T04` → `S3-T08 Terbit pipeline` and `S4-T08 / S3-T08 UAT ops (Kontrak Disahkan → Terbit)` → `S9 UAT acceptance`.
- **Answer store (reads by every report):** `S0-T02` → `S5-T15 single answer store` (Sprint 1) → the writers (`S5-T03`, `S3-T04`, `S3-T05`, `S3-T06`, `S7-T01`) and the readers (`S10-T01..T03`, `S6-T01`, `S6-T10`).
- **Chapter learning:** `S4-T02 DSKP` → `S4-T03 Bab mapping tool` (its first step is the investigation of the existing chapter data) → `S5-T02`, `S10-T04`, `S10-T05`.
- **Coin rate:** `S7-T05` (coin rate and reward backend: DM-48, DM-77, DM-78, API-ADM-16) lands in Sprint 2 and is read by `S5-T03`, `S3-T04` and `S7-T01`. Only `S7-T01` depends on it. `S5-T03` and `S3-T04` do NOT depend on it (L-8): until S7-T05 merges, or when no `reward_settings` row exists, they read the coin rate with the default 1, so Slice 0 needs no S7-T05. The reward screens are `S7-T11` in Sprint 5 (D113-continued: the former S7-T05 is split into a backend card and a screens card).
- **P0 portal-breakage branch** (`S0-T05 → S0-T06 → S10-T01..T03, S5-T06, S5-T10, S6-T01, S6-T02`) is independent and is the quickest visible win.

### 5.3 Split by number of developers
Assumptions: **1 ideal day = 1 day of focused work; a developer delivers 8 ideal days per 2-week sprint** (10 working days x 0.8 focus, after reviews, calls, rebasing). S = 1, M = 3, L = 6. Totals computed from the 118 cards: **Delivery A 369 ideal days for developers (plus 17 days of S9 verification, which is the Delivery Lead's), Delivery B 37 days (423 in all).** The figures are recomputed from the cards by the Delivery Lead whenever cards are added or split, and re-baselined after Sprint 1.

| Devs | Sector assignment (non-overlapping; days of Delivery A per developer) | Delivery A duration (days / 8 per sprint) | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | one at a time in this order: S0 → S8-T01 → S10 P0 → S6 P0 → S4 → S1 → S2 → S3 → S8 → S5 → S6 P1 → S7 (369 d) | about 47 sprints (94 weeks) | Not viable for 1.0. Use only if the user lowers the scope; then do P0 cards only (218 days, 28 sprints) |
| **2** | Dev A: S6, S5, S4, S10 (170 d). Dev B: S0, S1, S2, S3, S7, S8 (199 d) | about 25 sprints (50 weeks) | Possible but long |
| **3** | A: S6, S5 (117 d). B: S1, S2, S3, S10 (138 d). C: S0, S4, S7, S8 (114 d) | about 18 sprints (36 weeks) | Minimum recommended |
| **5** | A: S0 + S1 (71 d). B: S2 + S8 + S10 (84 d). C: S3 + S4 (61 d). D: S5 + S7 (79 d). E: S6 (74 d) | about 11 sprints (Sprint 0 + 10 = 21 weeks), then Delivery B | Recommended |

The sprint lists in §5A give the order of goals and cards. When a team's cards do not fit one sprint, the surplus follows in the next sprint (carry-over rules in §5A); Sprint 6 (hardening) starts when every Delivery A card is merged, not before. Scope is never cut to fit a date (D100).

Inside every sprint each developer works only in their own sectors' paths; a task needing another sector's path is a small cross-sector PR (§3; R1-START-HERE decision 7), reviewed within 1 working day by the team that holds that sector. The Delivery Lead (S9) is a separate role and does not count as a developer.

### 5A. Sprint plan

**Sprint length: 2 weeks (Sprint 0: 1 week).** Reasons: L cards are 6 ideal days, so they must fit inside one sprint; every sprint needs time for the UAT deploy and the teams' UAT acceptance runs (headed browser, three widths); the user sees a demo every 2 weeks, a rhythm they can follow. One week would put ceremonies and verification on the critical path; four weeks would hide a failing direction for too long. After Sprint 3, if velocity is stable and the user wants quicker feedback, switch to 1 week (this is an explicit retro decision).

**Ceremonies (all kept minimal).** Sprint planning 30 min (Delivery Lead + team leads: pick cards, confirm ACs, set the issue milestones). Daily: each team posts one report comment in #503 (§6.7), no meeting. Sprint review: the user watches the demo script live in a visible browser (≤30 min). Retro: 15 min, three bullets (stop/start/keep) posted as a comment in #503. Nothing else.

**"Sprint done" definition.** A sprint is done only when (1) every card in it is Done under DoD-T *or* explicitly carried over with a reason; (2) **every AC on the sprint's exit list has a Pass row** in the AC ledger (`[CI]` ACs in CI, `[UAT]` ACs on UAT, §6.9); (3) the demo was shown to the user; (4) `main` branch of work (`feature/v3-bijak-r1`) is green (CI, sidebar-walk). Progress is reported as "ACs passing / ACs planned", then "cards done / planned", in that order. Sprint-order rule: a card's dependencies (`deps:`) are in the same sprint or an earlier one, and a card starts only after its dependencies are merged; `S9-T01`, `S9-T02`, `S9-T05` and `S9-T06` are recurring Delivery Lead cards that run in every sprint (S9-T06 has no `deps:`: its helper lands in Sprint 1 and its chain runs follow as each chain lands); they have no sprint-order check of their own (their issues carry the milestone R1 Sprint 1, S9-T06 R1 Sprint 3).

**Carry-over rules.** A card that misses the sprint returns to Backlog with its progress note and is re-estimated; carry-over is allowed once; the second time it must be split or re-assigned at planning. A card whose AC fails on UAT returns to `status:in-progress` (it is not Done); it counts as carry-over. Anything a card needs from another sector and did not get is reported as blocked on day 2, not on the last day (comment in #503, label `needs:S<n>`).

**Tracking per sprint.** The board is the `r1` issues with their labels and milestones (`R1 Sprint 0..N`); sector, type, priority, delivery and status are labels (§6.1). Per sprint the Delivery Lead posts in #503: planned days, merged days, ACs planned, ACs passing. The AC ledger (`docs/plan/r1-ac-ledger.md`, §6.9) is the real measure.

#### Slice 0: one end-to-end chain first

For a team with limited time (about 24 hours of working time), build ONE chain end to end before anything else (D112(7)). It proves AC-CHAIN-1 and AC-CHAIN-2 (PRD 40 section X9). Everything else follows the normal sprints below; no card is dropped or changed by Slice 0, only ordered first. Cards are done under the usual rules (gap check first, §0B; DoD-T).

The cards, in order (each step needs the one before it; step 4a is done BEFORE step 4, because S1-T10 in step 4 depends on S1-T03 and S1-T04, run 6). The helper `coinsPerCorrect()` reads the `reward_settings` row when it exists and returns 1 when it does not, so Slice 0 does not include S7-T05 (L-8):

1. **Stimulus group 1..n, published and served as ONE unit.** S1-T01 (group invariants, 1..n members, FR-ADM-QE2.4) plus the group publish: S1-T02 (publish gate) and S2-T01 (group-first entry in the Studio wizard, the minimum needed to create "Rajah 3" and its members). Done when: AC-ADM-QE1.2, AC-ADM-QE2.1, AC-ADM-QE2.2.
2. **The conditional Bab/SP gate.** S1-T02 (FR-ADM-QE4.5: Bab before Terbit where the Subjek + Tingkatan has chapters; FR-ADM-QE4.6: SP/TP only where DSKP nodes exist) with S4-T02 (the DSKP loaded and verified for the subject used, U-OP-01). Done when: AC-ADM-QE4.3, AC-ADM-QE4.4, AC-ADM-QE4.5.
3. **Latih Tubi serves whole groups and writes `practice_answers`.** S1-T06 (group-safe assembly), S1-T07 (servability), S8-T02 (stimulus shown once, sticky), S4-T06 and S5-T01 (T1 to T5 served, which S5-T03 depends on; both come before S5-T03), S5-T03 (Latih Tubi) and S5-T15 (the single answer store with `mode`, DM-44); the reports read that store: S10-T01 (Prestasi), S10-T03 (Analisa Item). Done when: AC-ADM-QE7.3, AC-ADM-M06.2, and the AC-CHAIN-1 steps (a) to (d) with their Prestasi and Analisa Item numbers. This proves AC-CHAIN-1.
4. **ONE Sejarah Kertas 1 paper end to end** (Sejarah has sample papers and its DSKP loaded; Kertas 1 first, D112(4)): Kontrak S3-T01 (Disahkan, FR-ADM-Q16.*) -> Set Builder S3-T02 -> Semak & Terbit (S3-T08 for the Terbit pipeline; S1-T10 for the batch-to-paper step) -> pupil sitting and result S3-T04 (with S8-T03 answer widgets, S8-T04 navigator/timer/Hantar on phone) -> Prestasi and Analisa Item (S10-T01, S10-T03), with the answers written by S5-T15 (mode `simulasi`). Done when: AC-ADM-Q16.1, AC-ADM-Q16.3, AC-ADM-Q07.3 to AC-ADM-Q07.6, AC-MUR-13-1, AC-MUR-13-4, AC-MUR-13-6, AC-MUR-13-25.
4a. **Paper PDF start of AC-CHAIN-2, done before step 4 (S1-T03, S1-T04 and S2-T02 step 4a come before S1-T10)** (needed to prove AC-CHAIN-2 from its first step; S2-T02's tab `Kertas Diimport` and the batch button `Hantar untuk semakan` call the S1-T10 route, so that part of S2-T02 lands right after S1-T10 in step 4; if time runs out, the Sejarah paper may be entered by group-first manual entry (S2-T01) and these cards follow immediately after): S0-T03 (real Tesseract OCR, D112(2)), S1-T03 (extraction keeps groups and Bahagian), S1-T04 (timeout, error state), S2-T02 (Import & Semakan, Semak Ekstrak). Done when: AC-ADM-QE5.1, AC-ADM-QE5.2, AC-ADM-QE5.3, AC-ADM-Q07.1. This completes AC-CHAIN-2.

The final check runs AC-CHAIN-1 and AC-CHAIN-2 on UAT in a visible browser at 1280 px with a 375 px pass of the pupil steps, after the designated UAT developer deployed on request (card S9-T06 runs and accepts the chains; the spec of each chain is written by the last card of that chain, §4.S9; for Slice 0 they are run by hand with the AC-CHAIN-2 numbers applied to the Sejarah paper, by the teams that own the Slice 0 cards). The content for steps 1 to 4 is entered by Admin users (D112(4)).

### 5B. Capacity variants: the same backlog on 1, 2, 3 and 5 developers

The sector assignment per variant is the table in §5.3. Rules for every variant:
1. P0 cards of the sprint list in §5A come before any P1 card, whatever the variant.
2. The order of sprint *goals* is fixed (portal P0 and answer store → question entry → Bijak on phone → P1 → hardening → Delivery B), so fewer developers means more sprints per goal, not a different order.
3. Sector ownership stays disjoint: a team (or developer) holds whole sectors by lock, and a sector is never split between two teams at the same time. Exception: S5 and S6 may be split by sub-folder if the team that holds the sector agrees in a comment on the sector's mirror line in #502.
4. At 1 developer, the Delivery Lead decides the order after each sprint and the user is told the new expected date.
5. Sprint 0 for fewer than 5 developers: S0-T01..T04, S8-T01 first; S4-T01/T02 and the rest of Sprint 0 follow in Sprint 1.
